2026.11.11 晚报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-10T21:30:00+08:00 → 2026-11-11T21:30:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.11 22:01 北京时间 · 372 条

窗口：2026-11-10 21:30 至 2026-11-11 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为 2026-11-10 21:30 至 2026-11-11 21:30（北京时间，UTC+8）。其中 10 日 21:30 至 11 日 06:00 与本日早报《2026.11.11 - X Digest - 早报》窗口重叠：Gemini 4 Carbon 正式推送与任务成本比较、Anthropic IPO 定价下修和供应商认购、S-1 短期算力由盈转亏、台积电 10 月营收、TPU 8tx/8i Max、CoreWeave 三季度财报与 backlog、出口管制传闻以及 Stripe 收购 OpenRouter、Box 与 Claude 打通文件读写等，已在早报出现，本期仍完整收录以保持 24 小时覆盖；增量主线在 11 日 06:00 至 21:30（约对应美东周一晚至周二白天）：（1）卖方转述 Anthropic 路演/电话会称不延长与 SpaceX 的高价算力合同，并把约 100 亿美元训练成本归到整个 Claude 5.5 家族，同时路演视频给出毛利率与单位算力收入大幅改善的公司口径；（2）软银为美国 AI 数据中心电力设备改计划并确认约 37.3 亿美元亏损，WSJ 报道孙正义轮机预订“做过了”，CFO 称不缩小俄亥俄项目但要重新评估采购；（3）台积电董事会提高股息并批准 409.3 亿美元资本拨款，还批准最多各 5 亿美元认购 Anthropic 普通股与 OpenAI 优先股；管理层在日经论坛上称先进制程需求“极度饥渴”、未见放缓；（4）存储厂已被长期协议锁住相当比例的 2027–2030 年收入，但股价仍大幅回撤，移动 DRAM 还有新的涨价转述；KLA/Onto 则说内存涨价在侵蚀设备商毛利；（5）CoreWeave 称合格需求是产能的 10 倍并获得全额预付款，Sam Altman 则警告 neocloud 需求存在重复计算，真正大幅拉动需求的“只有 OpenAI 和 Anthropic”。以下“分析/含义”为本摘要的综合判断；凡标注“据报/传闻/作者观点/卖方转述”的内容均来自 X 帖子或其转述的媒体报道，未经独立核实，数字均以原帖为准。

一、Anthropic 卖方会：不续高价 SPCX 算力，把近端利润率和长期产能重新分开

卖方转述（非公司备案，@jukan05 称来自 Barclays 主办电话、分析师参加非交易路演后向投资人的通报）：Anthropic 强调自身比开源“更稳、人才更好”，并称 AI 是 winner-take-all，“只要执行得当就会通吃”；与 SpaceX 的合同“不会延长到明年年中之后”；训练成本方面，先说开发 Opus 5.5 超过 100 亿美元，随后更正：这 100 亿是整个 Claude 5.5 家族（Opus / Sonnet / Haiku 5.5）的训练支出，不是单模型。@jukan05 自己的 takeaway 是：他们低估了需求才向 SpaceX 租算力，现在宁愿多锁产能，以免再被动。@RihardJarc 补充合同结构：该高价算力协议一部分可在 1 月 27 日、另一部分可在 3 月 27 日取消；不应把这种价格当成长期默认，ANT 作为最大买方议价权最强。他进一步认为，连利润率最好、付得起最高价的买方都不愿按现价续约，意味着不能把现价写入 neocloud 模型；在利息负担下，这些模式的盈利能力变得可疑，市场已从“收入和 backlog”转向经济性。

路演视频中的公司口径（@AndrewCurran_ 摘录，属宣传主张，不是已审计财报）：过去六个季度收入增长超过 45 倍，算力与基础设施成本只增 10 倍，单位算力收入改善超过 4 倍；年初至今毛利率升至 85% 以上（2025 年同期 29%）；企业客户自年初增长 8 倍。同一段视频还称，Mythos 让 Claude 在几乎无人工干预下 3 天跑上 AMD GPU，内核几乎 100% 由 Claude 移植或编写——这是能力演示，不能外推成 CUDA 生态已经被替代（@benitoz 明确反驳“CUDA is dead”）。另有低关注账号转述彭博称 IPO 多倍超额认购；更低粉账号还称银行家正准备把估值目标上调至 1.5 万亿美元或更高——后一说没有可核验链接，只应视为传闻。名单内 @jukan05 白天也曾问“是不是又要调回 1.5 万亿或更高”，与这一传闻互相印证，但都不是定价已被上修的证据。@kimmonismus 称 IPO 计划在 11 月 19 日，并猜测 OpenAI 会把 GPT-6.2 Astra 放到上市前抢风头，同属作者判断/传闻。

台积电董事会的硬动作把上市与供应链绑在一起（@dnystedt，公司公告转述）：三季度股息提至纪录 NT$9/股（原 NT$7），并批准 409.3 亿美元资本拨款；另批准最多 5 亿美元设立半导体产业/生态投资公司，以及最多各 5 亿美元、按 IPO 价认购 Anthropic 普通股和按优先股认购 OpenAI。这与早报中的七家供应商 260 亿美元认购不是同一笔，而是台积电自己的追加授权。@ScroogeCap 估算 ANT 对 SpaceX 的合同约 31 亿美元/月、约 372 亿美元/年，占其引用的 SPCX 2027 年共识收入约 1810 亿美元的 20% 以上，认为股价可能因此波动，并称自己对 SPCX 做了很小的空头——这是作者估算和交易观察，不是公司指引。

含义：把“毛利率和单位算力收入改善”（若路演数字属实）和“不再按现价续短期高溢价算力、训练成本以十亿计”放在一起，IPO 卖的是增长、长期云/TPU 合同和供应商认购，不是把 SpaceX/neocloud 的现价当成稳态 COGS。对 $SPCX 和其他 neocloud，最大的高质量买方在明示价格上限和取消窗口；这并不能单独证伪它们的 backlog，但会压缩“高价算力可以按当前水平外推”的估值假设。不要把卖方纪要写成已经发出的终止通知，也不要把 1.5 万亿传闻写成新的定价区间。跟踪：S-1 后续修订是否写入不续约、1 月/3 月取消窗口是否被行使，以及指引口径与 10 月 ARR 的差异（有讨论提及年化约 770 亿美元的四季度指引对比 10 月 ARR 810 亿美元，来源仍是卖方转述）。

二、软银轮机/电力设备：亏损已经入账，被否的是机器人交易，不是俄亥俄项目本身

已发生的财务结果（多条互相印证，请以公司财报为准）：@anissagardizy8 与同事为《华尔街日报》报道，孙正义在 2025 年 AI 热潮中预订了燃气轮机市场的很大一块，“做过了”，赌注反噬。@nodiligence / @jasonschips 给出更完整的分项：软银为美国 AI 数据中心电力设备和工厂档期提前支付 94.8 亿美元，后改变计划，亏损 37.3 亿美元，其中 28.5 亿不可退还预付款、8.80 亿合同修改与和解，涉及包括俄亥俄园区在内的采购。关键词扫描中 @TFTC21 补充：软银当季亏损约 1.12 万亿日元（约 70 亿美元），对比分析师预期盈利 1290 亿日元，为近两年首次净亏；上年同期约盈利 160 亿美元；东京股价盘中最多跌 11%。彭博转述 CFO 后藤芳光：没有缩小俄亥俄项目的计划，但正在对包括采购策略在内的安排做“彻底重新评估”。GE Vernova 与 Bloom Energy 股价周二随消息下跌。

对照早报/重叠时段：软银 CFO 曾否认马斯克关于“轮机订单违约”的指控，GE 管理层也称未见取消。本期更准确的表述是“改计划 + 计提亏损 + 重新评估采购”，不是已经宣布停建俄亥俄或已确认对 GE 违约。@NewmoonCap 指出 GEV 三季报未提及此事，且已预订至 2031，认为档期可能已被再分配——这是作者推断。@nodiligence 同一篇还记录：GE Vernova 九个月预收款和递延收入 195 亿美元，超过同期 153 亿美元自由现金流，说明买方在设备发货前就用现金把队列扛下来；Bitdeer 把三个共 86.2MW 的 AI 站点再推迟一个季度，Knoxville 的延误来自电力和电气设备。谁先付款谁锁交期，但计划一变就要自己承担损失。

关键词扫描另有一条名单外信息（@ApollonVisual，约 400 粉，文中称 ABB 已确认）：中国外商投资安全审查工作机制否决软银拟议以 54 亿美元收购 ABB 机器人业务；中国约占 ABB Robotics 全球工业机器人出货量的一半。交易上周才披露。这与轮机减值是不同的监管/地缘事件，不能并成“软银 AI 计划全面失败”，但对 $SFTBY 和 ABB 机器人业务的中国收入有直接含义。

含义：需求叙事没有被单季减值自动证伪——CFO 仍说不缩俄亥俄——但预付款和排产计划可以迅速改写，已付定金不是已建电厂，更不是已并网的兆瓦。对 $SFTBY，这是资本配置和披露质量的近端打击；对 $GEV / $BE，市场把它读成设备需求的风险溢价，尽管部分档期可能只是易主而不是消失。对组合的含义仍是：能在自身资产负债表上等待、或已把预收款变成可见现金流的设备商，相对更依赖单一买方排产承诺的项目，不对称没有消失。

三、需求数据与论坛口径仍然偏强，出货下滑被指向短缺而不是需求消失

公司行动与管理层论坛（较硬的一层）：台积电董事会将三季度股息提至纪录 NT$9/股，并批准 409.3 亿美元资本拨款，以及最多各 5 亿美元参与 Anthropic IPO 和 OpenAI 优先股（见上节）。@aleabitoreddit / @dnystedt 转述日经论坛：台积电共同首席运营官张晓强称，先进制程需求“insatiable”（极度饥渴），客户“想抓住任何能到手的算力”；被问需求是否放缓时称“未见任何放缓迹象”；需求仍远高于当前供给能力；客户端瓶颈被指向电力和基板。对 2024–2029 年 AI 加速器收入 mid-to-high-50s 的年化增长估计，魏哲家称“可能还会更高”。这是论坛转述，不是新的正式财报指引，但来自通常措辞保守的公司。

出口与整机（媒体转述，@dnystedt）：韩国 11 月前 10 日半导体出口同比 +331.2% 至 166 亿美元，占全部出口 49.7%；整体出口 335 亿美元，对比上年同期 157 亿美元。全球服务器出货连续第二季下滑，三季度 -0.8% 至 463.8 万台，DigiTimes Research 归因于 CPU、内存和基板短缺，而非需求消失；预计四季度 +5.5% 至 489 万台；Meta 有望占四季度出货 19%（三季度 13.4%），首次超过亚马逊，谷歌第三、戴尔第四、微软第五；台湾厂商四季度全球份额预期 88.9%，广达约 25%。全年 2026 年服务器出货预计 1895 万台，同比 +14.2%，再创纪录。英业达库存升至 NT$1624 亿（约 49 亿美元），归咎零件错配影响现货订单交付。AMD 下一代 Helion agentic AI 机柜据报 11 月开始出货，台湾合作伙伴包括鸿海、广达、纬颖、英业达、技嘉。港交所已同意阿里推进平头哥（T-Head）分拆赴港上市，上市委批准和证监会备案仍待，规模未定。

中国数据中心成本（@SemiAnalysis_ 现场观察，不是经审计的行业普查）：参观远景乌兰察布 Galaxy 800MW 风电数据中心，首期 120MW 一年内从空地交付；中国 100MW 数据中心造价现低于 3 亿美元（不含 IT），约美国的五分之一，较 2021 年下降约 25%；固态变压器试点把 10kV 交流电转为 800V 直流电直供到机架（美国仍在路线图上）；黄仁勋称 Vera Rubin 不再需要水冷机组，该园区没有屋顶冷水机却仍保留大部分灵活性；内蒙古已宣布 25GW 数据中心项目；高峰用工 3000–4000 人，为美国典型工地的 3–5 倍。

含义：10 月营收、11 月上旬出口、论坛口径和资本拨款，都没有显示先进制程需求断裂；服务器出货连续两季下滑若主要是缺料，和“需求仍远高于供给”可以同时成立，全年仍被指向新高。中国造价差和用工密度说明，同样的 AI 负载在不同司法辖区的资本开支和交付周期不可比，不能用美国项目的延误去外推全球瓦数。不要把论坛上的“insatiable”或“可能更高”写成新的正式指引，也不要把缺料造成的出货下滑写成资本开支周期已经结束。跟踪：11–12 月台积电是否好于季节性、Nvidia 11 月 17 日财报对供给链和库存的表述，以及 Meta 是否真的在四季度成为最大服务器买家。

四、存储：长协已经锁住一块收入，股价却在给未锁部分定价；涨价开始传导到设备商毛利

合同与股价的错位（@nodiligence，数字引自美光 4Q26 业绩准备稿而非 10-K）：存储厂已提前卖掉未来几年很大一块，但股价仍被抛得像什么都没卖掉。美光较历史收盘高点约跌 40%，却有 26 份长期协议，覆盖 2030 年前收入的 35% 以上，背后约 320 亿美元客户承诺，且多为现金定金。SK 海力士在首尔较 6 月高点几乎跌 60%，已与 14 家客户签约、锁定其 2027 和 2028 年产能的一半以上。多数合同带有价格下限和上限；已经交了现金定金的客户最不可能先行离开。作者认为，市场像是把“未被合同覆盖的那一段悬崖”当成了整家公司；若 AI 订单下滑，未锁定部分会先且快地重新定价，但锁定部分仍会按下限或之上开票。海力士四季度报告约在 1 月下旬，才能验证 DRAM 出货是否达到公司指引的中段 20% 左右增长。

价格与设备商（转述，需折扣）：@TradexWhisperer 引 Counterpoint Research，移动 DRAM 价格 2027 年一季度将涨 30%，二季度再涨 10%，而且是叠加上涨、以新的更高基数停留。关键词扫描中 Grok 回复称，消费级 32GB DDR5 从去年约 150 美元涨到起步 440–480 美元（中位数更接近 560 美元以上），单颗合约价涨逾 400%，短缺被预期持续到 2028–2030——这是模型整理，不是报价单。另一侧，@SKundojjala 指出 KLA 与 Onto 都说 DRAM 涨价在侵蚀毛利率：KLA 一季度已提示，并将持续到 2027、约 100 个基点；两者都在改设计以少用内存。检验/量测公司毛利率本就高于蚀刻、薄膜和光刻同行，但这是涨价从存储厂利润向设备商成本的再分配。@ShanghaoJin 从渠道侧观察到：DRAM 一级渠道商囤货“全死光”、零售已经卖不动，年初靠便宜长协货赚到的钱被“平准”；并以此对照英特尔“零售卖不动就不透支催渠道”。

3D DRAM 与中国供给（对照早报传闻）：早报关键词抽样曾转述 The Information，称 Nvidia 要求台积电起草制造 3D DRAM 的计划。本期 @dnystedt 转述原长江存储/华亚科董事长 Charles Kau 的表态：“台积电做不了 3D DRAM”，因为逻辑芯片与 DRAM 存储单元的制程差异极大；存储的大威胁是中国，其存储厂将在约两年后（2028 年末）爬坡多座新厂、冲击价格并改写行业格局。这是业者口头划界，不是台积电或 Nvidia 的正式确认或否认。@damnang2 与一位存储/设备老兵的专家电话，核心问题是：下一代 DRAM（4F²、3D DRAM）何时才有经济性——实验室能做，不代表良率、成本和客户需求已经支撑放量。

含义：长协和定金降低了 2027–28 年出货“归零”的概率，但不能自动修复估值——市场给的是未锁定部分和 2029 年以后利润率的折扣，这与早报中摩根士丹利下调海力士长期营业利润率假设是同一类担忧。涨价对 $MU / $SKHY 是利润，对 $KLAC / Onto 和下游手机/PC 是成本；渠道“卖不动”说明涨价已经开始遇到终端阻力，和“合约价还要再涨”可以同时存在。3D DRAM 仍是路线图争论，不是近端产能。跟踪：海力士 1 月季报的出货与指引、美光对 SCA 覆盖率的下一次更新，以及消费电子品牌是否开始把内存成本转嫁到整机价格。

五、Neocloud 的定价权、预付款和 Altman 的“重复计算”；TPU 分发开始写出第二需求池

CoreWeave 与预付款（对已披露财报的增量读法，不是新的官方数字）：@aleabitoreddit 转述电话会——合格需求是可用产能的 10 倍；对 2028 年产能的兴趣已是其可用量的数倍；7 月涨价 25%，三季度后又加 10%，四季度还要再加；Ampere 和 Hopper 等较旧 GPU 租金环比上涨 30–50%；融资条件改善（更高预付款、新贷款利息 7–7.5%）；并指引 2028 年上半年实现经调整净利润转正。同一账号称公司获得 100% 合同价值预付款，认为对 $NBIS / $IREN 是正面读法。@jasonschips 则提醒：需求不是问题，但单季净利息 8.46 亿美元，对照经营利润 2.07 亿美元，融资经济性仍是焦点。@GavinSBaker：GPU 集群融资从 offtake 转向预付款，使经营现金流/EBITDA 成为最重要指标；尽调负担从私募信贷评估 offtaker 信用，转到真正使用 GPU 的客户支付近乎 100% 的预付款；对客户而言，交付到上线和集群历史稳定性将是最关键指标，这也显著改善 GPU 的回收期。

Altman 对三星高管的问答（@jukan05 转述韩国媒体，不是公司备案）：谈及 neocloud 泡沫时，Altman 称“存在一定的需求重复计算”；被问未来五到十年 AI、内存和数据中心需求会否放缓，他答“短周期有起伏”；“真正在大幅拉动需求的只有 OpenAI 和 Anthropic，因此可能会出现供给冲击和一段动荡”。长期他仍认为 AI 需求会极大增长，token 用量将随实时写软件、机器人、航天和科研而急剧上升；未来几年最大约束是芯片供应链和数据中心，“需要加大对供应链的投资”。被问持续重资本开支是否压制盈利时，他答：“关键正在这里。如果我们能以比任何人都低的成本建成垂直整合的基础设施，并以薄利高量卖 token，就能领先对手。”“市场足够大，商品业务的通常规则并不适用，只要满足全球 token 需求就足以支撑一个繁荣的行业。”@zephyr_z9 把这段读成“中长期替代 Nvidia 的计划”。Altman 还称希望与三星做智能手机之后的 AI 设备，并对 AI 眼镜在社交场景中“挡在人与人之间”表示不适。

Google TPU 的分发（@PhotonCap 整理，部分为预测/未证实）：谷歌已把 TPU 客户群扩至近 100 家；甲骨文计划 2027 年起在 OCI 上提供 Google TPU；Counterpoint 预测 2024–2028 年 AI ASIC 的 HBM 比特需求将增至 35 倍，单颗平均 HBM 容量约增 5 倍；三星与海力士据报给 Google TPU 供 HBM；博通与谷歌的下一代 TPU 合作协议至 2031 年。有报道称谷歌可能扩大直接采购 HBM，并瞄准 15–20% 的成本下降，但规模和目标均未证实。Google Cloud 还将 Arm 架构 Axion 用作 TPU 8i Max 的主机 CPU，称较可比 x86 有 2 倍性价比。OCS 方面，$LITE 把光交换和泵浦激光作为 FY Q1 2027 主要增长来源，管理层认为 OCS 可能成为数十亿美元级年收入业务；谷歌称 Jupiter 的 OCS+SDN 使网络资本开支降 30%、功耗降 41%；微软 2025 年加入 OCP OCS 倡议。这些是公司/会议表述的整理，不是已经实现的收入。

含义：预付款、涨价和 10 倍合格需求，说明至少这一家 GPU 云的买方仍在用现金锁产能；这与 Altman 的“重复计算 / 供给冲击”和 Anthropic 不续高价 SPCX，是同一张图上的对冲，而不是互相取消。谁能把 backlog 换成可见现金（预付款或建成后的债券/ABS），谁才能在利息负担下活过 refinancing 日期——这与 @nodiligence 的融资地图是同一条逻辑。TPU 客户近百家、OCI 2027 年转售，则把 HBM 需求从“几乎只跟 Nvidia 绑定”写成第二个采购池，对三星/海力士是多元化，对 Nvidia 是份额而非总量问题。不要把 100% 预付款外推成全行业通缩风险已经消失，也不要把 Altman 的“薄利高量卖 token”当成已经实现的公司模型。跟踪：CRWV 预付款是否在现金流表上可复核、OCI TPU 的实际上线时间，以及 Google 是否真的绕过博通直接买 HBM。

要点：$MU $SKHY

存储厂商已经提前卖掉了未来几年中很大一块产量，而股票却被抛得像它们什么都没卖掉一样。

美光距离其历史收盘高点大约下跌了 40%，它有 26 份长期协议，覆盖到 2030 年超过 35% 的营收，背后约有 320 亿美元的客户承诺，其中大部分是现金定金。SK hynix 在首尔的股价较 6 月高点下跌了近 60%，已与 14 家客户签约，锁定其 2027 和 2028 年超过一半的产能。

这些协议大多带有价格下限和上限，而且已经交纳现金定金的客户，是最不可能违约走开的。

市场看起来像是在把未签约那部分的悬崖，当成整个公司来定价。如果 AI 订单下滑，未签约部分会首先、并且很快重新定价，但已签约部分仍会按地板价或更高的价格继续开票。

2026-11-11 21:19 北京时间 ｜ Source: @RihardJarc ｜ 原帖链接

这表明 $ANT——这个因为利润率最好而付得起最高算力价格的买方——不愿意按现在这些价格买算力。这意味着你不能把这些算力价格水平建模给 neocloud；再加上债务利息，这些商业模式的盈利能力就变得可疑。市场不想看到 neocloud 去做那些业务指标和利润说不通的大额交易。在 neocloud 这件事上，市场已经走过了只看“收入和 backlog”的阶段，现在紧盯经济学本身，而这件事对此没有帮助。

原文：It shows that $ANT, the buyer who can afford to pay the highest price compute-wise because it has the best margin, is not willing to pay compute prices at these levels. This means that you can't model these compute price levels for the neoclouds, and with debt interest, the business models become questionable in terms of profitability. The market doesn't want neoclouds doing big-volume deals with business metrics and profits that don't make sense. The market, when it comes to neoclouds, has moved past the "revenue and backlog" days and is laser-focused on economics and this doesn't help that.所回复原帖（@JaeKim1089208）：@RihardJarc Even if what Jukan said is true, how does this put additional pressure on neoclouds? 2026-11-11 11:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$MU $SKHY

存储厂商已经提前卖掉了未来几年中很大一块产量，而股票却被抛得像它们什么都没卖掉一样。

美光距离其历史收盘高点大约下跌了 40%，它有 26 份长期协议，覆盖到 2030 年超过 35% 的营收，背后约有 320 亿美元的客户承诺，其中大部分是现金定金。SK hynix 在首尔的股价较 6 月高点下跌了近 60%，已与 14 家客户签约，锁定其 2027 和 2028 年超过一半的产能。

这些协议大多带有价格下限和上限，而且已经交纳现金定金的客户，是最不可能违约走开的。

市场看起来像是在把未签约那部分的悬崖，当成整个公司来定价。如果 AI 订单下滑，未签约部分会首先、并且很快重新定价，但已签约部分仍会按地板价或更高的价格继续开票。

等到 SK hynix 在 1 月下旬发布四季报时，我们就会知道 DRAM 出货量是否像其指引的那样，实现了中段 20% 左右的增长。

原文：$MU $SKHY

memory makers have sold a big chunk of the next few years in advance, and the stocks are getting dumped like they sold nothing.

micron is about 40% off its record close, and it has 26 long-term deals covering more than 35% of revenue through 2030, backed by about $32bn of customer commitments that are mostly cash deposits. SK hynix's shares in seoul are almost 60% below their june peak, with 14 customers signed for more than half its 2027 and 2028 capacity.

most of those deals come with a floor and a ceiling on price, and a customer who's put down a cash deposit is the last one to walk away.

the market looks like it's pricing the cliff on the part that isn't contracted as if it were the whole company. if AI orders slip, the uncontracted part reprices first and fast, but the contracted part keeps billing at or above its floor.

when SK hynix reports its q4 report in late january, that's when we'll know whether DRAM shipments came in up in the mid-20% range like it guided.

2026-11-11 18:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

在美国以外，我这张资金地图上的 269 笔 AI 数据中心融资里，只有 28 笔显示出任何现金。在美国则是 143 笔中的 49 笔，而这 49 笔里有 40 笔是投资者在第一天就付钱的债券或 ABS。

美国看起来更好，主要是因为它有这类资产的债券市场。弗吉尼亚或得州的一座数据中心一旦建成并租出去，就可以再融资成债券或 ABS，一旦做成，现金就变得可见。美国以外，这 269 笔融资里有 151 笔是银行贷款，而银行贷款几乎从不说明已提取了多少。

东南亚是最清楚的例子。马来西亚、泰国和印尼在我的地图上合计有 89 个站点，它们的 39 笔融资没有一笔显示出现金。韩国是 30 笔中的 2 笔，日本 12 笔中的 1 笔，澳大利亚 17 笔中的 1 笔，德国 14 笔中的 1 笔。

今年秋天卡住的那些美国贷款，至少在大楼建成后还有一个债券市场可以去。这里很多项目没有显而易见的这类出口，所以贷款很可能更长时间地留在当初放贷的那家银行手里。

很难知道那里坐着多少建设风险，因为这恰好是交易外部的人看不到的那一部分。但这项建设离美国越远，可见度就越低，其中也就有越多可能最终留在银行资产负债表上，而且没有明显的退出路径。

原文：outside the US, only 28 of the 269 AI data-centre financings in my money map show any cash at all. in the US it’s 49 of 143, and 40 of those 49 are bonds or ABS that investors paid for on day one.

the US looks better mostly because it has a bond market for this stuff. once a data centre in virginia or texas is finished and leased, it can get refinanced into bonds or ABS, and the moment it does, the cash becomes visible. outside the US, 151 of the 269 financings are bank loans, and bank loans almost never say how much was drawn.

southeast asia is the clearest case. malaysia, thailand and indonesia have 89 sites in my map between them, and none of their 39 financings show cash. south korea is 2 of 30, japan 1 of 12, australia 1 of 17 and germany 1 of 14.

the US loans that got stuck this autumn at least have a bond market to go to once the building is done. a lot of these don’t have an obvious one, so the loan probably stays with whichever bank wrote it for a lot longer.

it’s hard to know how much construction risk is sitting there, because that’s exactly the part nobody outside the deal can see. but the further this build gets from the US, the less visible it gets, and the more of it probably ends up on a bank balance sheet with no obvious exit.引用原帖（@nodiligence）：credit markets still look calm about AI, probably because most of the build isn’t in them yet. it’s in short bank loans waiting for a bond to pay them off, and power and permits keep pushing that back. three of the biggest are already stuck with banks. https://t.co/A722Vr0dOa 链接

2026-11-11 19:41 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$MS $GS $JPM $DB $SMFG $MUFG $BNP $ACA $ORCL

我追踪了地图上每家大银行所在的每一笔 AI 数据中心融资，并统计有多少笔能看到任何人都可以核实的现金。这些银行按商业模式分得相当清楚。

德意志银行、摩根士丹利、摩根大通和高盛，有 41% 到 53% 的交易显示出现金。他们经手的很多是自己安排并直接卖给投资者的债券，所以先拿费用，再把风险转出去。

SMBC 和 MUFG 分别有 27% 和 21% 的交易显示出现金，贷款占其交易的 62% 和 85%。Natixis、BNP Paribas 和 Crédit Agricole 只有 0% 到 13%，贷款占比为 76% 到 100%。这些银行是放贷并持有，一旦停滞，卖不掉的部分——也就是建设那一块——就留在自己手里。

合计来看，日资或法资银行手上有 950 亿美元看不到已提取金额的美元贷款。MUFG 牵头了 Oracle 380 亿美元的 Vantage 方案，是 180 亿美元 Jupiter 贷款的代理行，并向得州与 Anthropic 相关的 Nexus 过桥投入了约 20 亿美元。这样一来，新墨西哥的电力延误和得州的许可冻结，就落在同一张资产负债表上。而 SMBC 自己的 James Martin 在 10 月对路透表示，投资者更青睐更接近完工的数据中心交易，这就会把离完工最远的那些留在持有人手里。

这些银行想退出时，可以做风险转移，付钱让别人承担第一损失。Octus 在 7 月报道称，买方往往是已经持有同一栋楼股权的基金，价格在 7% 到 9%，比其他基础设施的 8% 到 12% 更紧。风险最终落到已经拥有该资产的人身上，而定价假设大楼会被建成，并且离任何监管者更远。

监管已经开始关注。日本金融厅（FSA）已在计划检查美国数据中心项目融资，欧洲央行至少从 2 月起就在询问欧洲贷款人的敞口。不过两家都不会按银行公布已提取金额，所以从外部很难知道任何一家银行被卡住的那一块到底有多大。

如果想通过银行获得 AI 融资敞口，我更偏好把票据卖出去的那些，而不是把票据留在自己账上的那些。

原文：$MS $GS $JPM $DB $SMFG $MUFG $BNP $ACA $ORCL

i tracked every AI data-centre financing each big bank sits on in my map and counted how many show cash anyone can check. the banks split pretty cleanly by business model.

deutsche bank, morgan stanley, jpmorgan and goldman show cash on 41% to 53% of their deals. a lot of what they touch is bonds they arranged and sold straight on to investors, so they take the fee up front and pass the risk on.

SMBC and MUFG show cash on 27% and 21%, with loans at 62% and 85% of their deals. natixis, BNP paribas and crédit agricole are at 0% to 13%, with loans at 76% to 100%. these banks lend and hold, and in a stall they keep whatever doesn’t sell, which is the construction piece.

between them, japanese or french banks sit on $95bn of the US-dollar loans with no drawn figure. MUFG led oracle’s $38bn vantage package, is an agent on the $18bn jupiter loan, and put about $2bn into the anthropic-linked nexus bridge in texas. that puts a power delay in new mexico and a permit freeze in texas on the same balance sheet. and SMBC’s own james martin told reuters in october that investors favour data-centre deals closer to completion, which leaves the holders with whatever is furthest from it.

when these banks want out, they can do a risk transfer and pay someone to take the first losses. octus reported in july that the buyers are often funds that already own equity in the same buildings, at 7% to 9%, tighter than the 8% to 12% on other infrastructure. the risk ends up with someone who already owns the asset, at a price that assumes the building gets finished, and further from any regulator.

the regulators are starting to look. japan’s FSA is already planning inspections of US data-centre project finance, and the ECB has been asking european lenders about their exposure since at least february. neither publishes drawn amounts by bank, though, so from outside it’s hard to know how big any one bank’s stuck piece really is.

if you want AI financing exposure through banks, i prefer the ones that sell the paper over the ones that keep it.引用原帖（@nodiligence）：credit markets still look calm about AI, probably because most of the build isn’t in them yet. it’s in short bank loans waiting for a bond to pay them off, and power and permits keep pushing that back. three of the biggest are already stuck with banks. https://t.co/A722Vr0dOa 链接

@GavinSBaker（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 2 条）

2026-11-11 07:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @GavinSBaker ｜ 原帖链接

融资方式从用 offtake 协议为 GPU 集群融资，转向 prepayment，这意味着 OCF to EBITDA 成了最重要的指标。

尽职调查的负担，也从评估 offtaker 信用资质的私募信贷基金，转移到实际使用 GPU、并支付 prepayment 的客户身上。

对于越来越多近乎 100% 预付的客户来说，dock to live（从到货到上线）以及历史集群稳定性水平，将是最重要的指标。

这也显著改善了 GPU 的回收期。

原文：The shift away from financing GPU clusters with offtake agreements to prepayments means that OCF to EBITDA is the most important metric.

Moves the diligence burden from private credit funds evaluating the creditworthiness of offtaker to the actual customer of the GPU making the prepayment.

Dock to live and historic cluster stability levels will be the most important metrics to customers who are increasingly paying nearly 100% upfront.

Also significantly improves the payback period on GPUs.

@buccocapital（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 8 条）

2026-11-11 01:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @buccocapital ｜ 原帖链接

我不会这样概括企业 AI 采用的现状。广义上，衡量一个组织里 AI 的 ROI 有两个变量：
1. 你在自动化或增强的是什么任务
2. 这家企业到底清不清楚自己为什么能成功？

#1 任务。
举个很简单的例子：关闭一张客户支持工单。大多数公司按渠道知道这要花多少钱——比如电话 40 美元、邮件 15 美元、聊天 5 美元。这是用坐席薪资、支持他们的编制、所用技术、场地，以及差旅和培训费用算出来的。

现在想象你能用 AI *成功地*回答一个问题，而你用的那家 AI 公司为此收 70 美分。好，在这个具体、边界清晰的用例里，你非常清楚 AI 的 ROI。

再看其他任务。你推出新产品的速度。更模糊一些，但要判断 1）开发者生产力是否提高、2）这是否把收入曲线的斜率往上掰，也并非离谱的任务。

再往曲线远端走。现在能用品牌设计资产生成 21 个不同版本的营销创意；让 40% 的分析请求变成自助，而不再卡在等人回答的队列里。要直接测量 ROI，就混乱得多、更难直接衡量。

#2 公司是否把事理顺了？

他们是否知道到底是什么在推动业务向前？我认为很多企业的答案其实是：不知道。他们是否可靠地知道谁是顶尖贡献者？是否知道真正驱动收入增长的那两三个输入？不仅知道，能否加快它们？如果你问公司里 10 个人“对公司最重要的事是什么”，他们会给出同一个答案吗？同样，我觉得大多是否定的。

如果你不能深入理解自己的业务、它的驱动力、以及谁在驱动它，就很难知道 AI 是否在帮你。
----------
TL;DR：有些任务很容易衡量 ROI，有些则不然。有些把事理顺了的企业很容易衡量 ROI，有些则不然。

（回复/引用 @TheStalwart 关于“不知道这些支出有没有回报，但员工会因为工具被拿走而崩溃”的概括）

原文：I wouldn't characterize the state of enterprise AI adoption this way. Broadly, I would say there are two variables in measuring the ROI of AI in an organization:
1. The task you are automating or augmenting
2. Does this business have any fucking idea why they are successful?

#1 The task.
Let's take a very simple example. Closing out a support ticket from a customer. Most companies know how much this costs by channel - say, $40 to pick up the phone, $15 to answer it by email, $5 by chat. This is calculating using things like the agent salaries, the headcount that supports them, the technology they use, the facilities, and any travel and training expenses

Now imagine you can use AI to *successfully* answer a question and the AI company you use charges you 70 cents for that. OK, you really clearly know the ROI of AI in this specific, bounded, use case.

Consider other tasks. The velocity with which you ship new products. A bit fuzzier but not a crazy task to determine whether 1) Developer productivity has increased 2) Whether that is bending the slope of your revenue curve upward.

Farther out on the curve. Being able to now generate 21 different versions of a marketing campaign's creative using your brand design assets. Making 40% of analytics requests self-service instead of being stuck in a queue for humans to answer. Quite a bit messier to measure direct ROI.

#2 Does the company have it's shit together?

Do they know what is actually driving the business forward? I think that the answer for many businesses is actually, No. Do they reliably know who their top performers are? Do they know the actual 2-3 inputs that drive revenue growth? Not only do they know them but can they accelerate them? If you asked 10 people at the company what "the most important thing" was for the business, would they give the same answer? Again, I think the answer is mostly No.

If you do not deeply understand your business, what is driving it, and who is driving it, it is quite hard to know whether AI is helping you.
----------
TL;DR. For some tasks it is quite easy to measure the ROI. For others, less so. For some businesses who have their shit together, it is quite easy to measure the ROI. For others, less so.引用原帖（@TheStalwart）：Would it be fair to characterize the state of enterprise AI adoption as:

"We don't really know if we're getting any return from all this spending, but our employees would lose their shit if we took the tools away?" 链接

在与三星高管的问答中，Sam Altman 回应了关于 neocloud 板块泡沫的担忧，称“需求存在一些重复计算。”

当被问到未来五到十年，对 AI、存储芯片和数据中心的需求是否可能走平或增速放缓时，他回答：“短期内会有起伏。”

Altman 说：“真正在大幅拉动需求增长的，只有 OpenAI 和 Anthropic 两家，所以我们可能会看到一次供给冲击，以及一段动荡期。”

原文：During a Q&A with Samsung executives, Sam Altman addressed concerns about a bubble in the neocloud sector, saying, “There has been some double counting of demand.”

Asked whether demand for AI, memory chips, and data centers could level off or grow more slowly over the next five to ten years, he replied, “There are ups and downs in the short term.”

“OpenAI and Anthropic are the only two companies really driving substantial growth in demand, so we could see a supply shock and a period of turbulence,” Altman said.

Sam Altman 说：“长期来看，对 AI 的需求会极大增长。” 他预期，随着 AI 实时编写定制软件，并更广泛地用于机器人、航天器和科学研究，AI 模型处理的信息单位——tokens 的用量将急剧上升。

Altman 说：“未来几年 AI 扩张的最大约束，将是芯片供应链和数据中心基础设施。我们需要加大对供应链的投资。”

三星高管问，OpenAI 持续的大规模投资是否在给盈利能力带来压力，Altman 回答：“这就是关键。如果我们能以比任何人更低的成本建成垂直整合的基础设施，并以薄利高量出售 tokens，我们就能跑在竞争前面。”

Altman 说：“这个市场太大了，商品业务的通常规则并不适用。只要能满足全球对 tokens 的需求，就足以撑起一个繁荣的行业。”

原文：“Demand for AI will grow enormously over the long term,” Sam Altman said. He expects the use of tokens—the units of information AI models process—to rise sharply as AI writes custom software in real time and finds wider use in robotics, spacecraft, and scientific research.

“The biggest constraints on AI expansion over the next few years will be the chip supply chain and data center infrastructure,” Altman said. “We need more investment in the supply chain.”

Asked by Samsung executives whether OpenAI’s continued heavy investment was putting pressure on profitability, Altman replied, “That’s the crux of it. If we can build vertically integrated infrastructure at a lower cost than anyone else and sell tokens in high volumes at thin margins, we can get ahead of the competition.”

“The market is so large that the usual rules of a commodity business don’t apply,” Altman said. “Just meeting global demand for tokens would be enough to build a thriving industry.”所回复原帖（@jukan05）：During a Q&A with Samsung executives, Sam Altman addressed concerns about a bubble in the neocloud sector, saying, “There has been some double counting of demand.”

SpaceX 正在开发 Kessel，这是一款定制的 Vera Rubin NVL72 机柜，用被动 PCB midplane 取代内部的 NVLink 线缆匣。它保留 Oberon 的 72 个 GPU 封装、18 个计算托盘和 9 个交换托盘，但把它们背靠背重新排列在这块板周围。可以把它想成迷你版 Kyber，scale-up 域更小，设计意在同时用于地面和轨道部署。问题在于，这个更小的版本能否让 midplane 变得切实可行。（1/4）🧵

Think mini Kyber, with a smaller scale-up domain and a design intended for both terrestrial and orbital deployment. The question is whether this smaller version can make the midplane practical. $CRWV 财报及对 $NBIS 和其他 Neocloud 的 read through：

“我们合格需求是可用产能的 10 倍。”

- 对 2028 年产能的兴趣，已经相当于 CoreWeave 可用量的数倍

- CoreWeave 在 7 月提价 25%，三季度晚些时候再加 10%，四季度还会再提

- Ampere 和 Hopper 租赁价格环比上涨 30–50%（较老的 GPU）

- 融资条件改善（更高的 prepayment，新贷款利率降至 7–7.5%）

- 2028 年上半年实现正的调整后净利润

所以我们看到定价在走高，尤其是较老的 GPU……融资机制改善，以及极端的需求失衡：需求是供给的 10 倍。

这家公司本身在利率上仍有同样的问题，但我认为其他 Neocloud 处境更好。

不过，眼看着 AI 基础设施公司股价暴跌、而需求还在增长，还是很疯狂。

原文：$CRWV ER and read through for $NBIS and other Neoclouds:

“We have 10x as much qualified demand as available capacity”.

- interest in 2028 capacity already amounts to several times what CoreWeave has available

- CoreWeave raised prices by 25% in July, +10% later in Q3, and more to come in Q4

- Ampere and Hopper rental pricing rose 30–50% quarter over quarter (older GPUs)

- better financing conditions (higher prepayments, lower interest 7–7.5% for new loans)

- positive adjusted net income in the first half of 2028.

So we’re seeing higher pricing esp. for older GPUs…better financing mechanics, and extreme demand imbalance where you have 10x demand > supply.

Same issues are there for this company in specific with interest rates, but I’d argue other Neoclouds are in a better spot.

But it’s wild to witness AI infrastructure names crash while demand keeps growing

> 这也强化了 Arm 在 AI 基础设施中的战略定位：host 计算效率会直接影响规模化条件下的数据准备、工作负载编排和 agentic reasoning 任务。

- Agent Substrate on GKE now natively supports Axion, enabling 10x higher sandbox density compared to standard container runtimes for scaled agentic AI workload deployment.

> OCS 竞争正从相对静态的光路，扩展到动态的 AI fabric。

> 端到端重配置时间和网络软件成熟度，比孤立的器件切换速度更重要。

- $COHR 提供基于数字液晶的 OCS。

- 其配置从 64x64 到 512x512 端口。

- 公司已演示过 300x300 端口系统。

> 端口成本、插入损耗、重配置时间和客户采用情况，将塑造 $LITE 与 $COHR 之间的竞争。

> 核心变化是：OCS 正在从 Google 以 MEMS 为中心的架构向外多样化。

> MEMS 在高端口数和低损耗上有吸引力，而 SiPh 则瞄准更快的重配置和半导体制造的可扩展性。

> 投资者应跟踪 $LITE 实际的 OCS 营收增长、$COHR 的客户采用，以及 $TSEM 向放量制造的过渡。

- $LITE reported significantly improved OCS demand visibility.

- Voyant plans to supply OCS engines rather than complete networking systems.

- Key development priorities include non-blocking topology, optical loss, crosstalk, and packaging.

> LiDAR photonic switching technology is expanding into AI data center infrastructure.

> Fast device switching alone does not guarantee better network throughput. Scheduling and reconfiguration overhead remain critical.

- OCP published a Fast-Switching OCS white paper in 2026.

- Contributors include $AMD, $INTC, $CIEN, $LITE, iPronics, and Lumotive.

- An April 2026 OCP paper compared MEMS and SiPh switching technologies.

> OCS competition is expanding from relatively static optical paths toward dynamic AI fabrics.

> End-to-end reconfiguration time and network software maturity matter more than isolated device switching speed.

- $COHR offers digital liquid crystal-based OCS.

- Its configurations range from 64x64 to 512x512 ports.

- The company has demonstrated a 300x300-port system.

> OCS competition extends beyond MEMS versus SiPh, with liquid crystal emerging as another high-radix option.

> Port cost, insertion loss, reconfiguration time, and customer adoption will shape competition between $LITE and $COHR.

> The central shift is OCS diversification beyond Google's MEMS-centered architecture.

> MEMS offers attractive high-port-count and low-loss characteristics, while SiPh targets faster reconfiguration and semiconductor manufacturing scalability.

> Investors should monitor $LITE's actual OCS revenue growth, $COHR's customer adoption, and $TSEM's transition into volume manufacturing.

- $GOOGL 在 2010 年代开始开发 OCS，并将其部署在 Jupiter 数据中心网络和 TPU 基础设施中。

- Google 称，其由 OCS 和 SDN 赋能的 Jupiter 架构，使网络 CAPEX 降低 30%，功耗降低 41%。

- OCS 在交换层重新配置光连接，而无需把光信号转换成电信号。

- $MSFT 于 2025 年加入 OCP 的 OCS 倡议，与 Google、$NVDA、$LITE 和 $COHR 并列。

> 不断增大的 AI 集群，正在暴露电分组交换在功耗、容量和成本上的约束。

- $AMZN 于 2026 年 5 月推出基于 ShuffleBox 的扁平网络架构，但这种无源光互连方法与 OCS 并不相同。

- OCS reconfigures optical connections without converting optical signals into electrical signals at the switching layer.

- $MSFT joined the OCP OCS initiative alongside Google, $NVDA, $LITE, and $COHR in 2025.

- $META developed Disaggregated Scheduled Fabric to address the scaling limitations of traditional Clos networks and is exploring alternative switching technologies.

- $META continues to rely heavily on Ethernet packet switching in its Prometheus infrastructure, disclosed in October 2026.

> A wholesale transition by $MSFT, $AMZN, and $META to all-optical switching is not yet established by public disclosures.

> However, rising electrical packet switching costs strengthen the economic case for replacing selected switching layers with OCS.

> The OCS market opportunity could expand dramatically if adoption extends beyond Google to multiple hyperscalers.

> $LITE and $COHR offer direct OCS hardware exposure, while SiPh-based architectures could create additional manufacturing opportunities for $TSEM.

2026-11-11 03:36 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PhotonCap ｜ 原帖链接

- Xanadu（$XNDU）与 HyperLight 于 11 月 10 日宣布，TFLN 光开关的插入损耗为 7 mdB。

- 以 dB 计的损耗较 2025 年宣布的约 20 mdB 基准下降了 65%。

- 把所报告数字换成光子损耗，大约是每次开关通过 0.46% 对 0.16%。

- 两家公司称，在 HyperLight 的 TFLN Chiplet 规模晶圆制造线上实现了高良率和可重复性。

- 新闻稿未给出数值良率、样本量、开关速度、消光比，以及封装损耗是否计入。

- TFLN 是薄膜铌酸锂，利用普克尔效应通过电场改变折射率，以实现快速光学控制。

- Xanadu 另外报告，光纤到芯片耦合损耗平均为每端面 0.085 dB。

- HyperLight 在 2026 年 3 月宣布与 $UMC 和 Wavetek 合作，进行 6 英寸和 8 英寸 TFLN 代工生产。

> 量子信息无法通过传统光放大从损耗中恢复，所以更低的开关损耗直接保全到达计算的光子。

> 仅假设 100 次相同的开关通过，计算得到的透过率从 63.1% 升至 85.1%；该示意未计入实际系统的路由深度和所有其他损耗。

> 同样的开关损耗预算大约能容纳 2.9 倍的通过次数，但这并不意味着逻辑量子比特或计算性能增加 2.9 倍。

> 单独报告的每端面 85 mdB 耦合损耗，大约相当于新开关的 12 次通过，因此封装和芯片间连接仍是降低总光路损耗的核心。

> 在 AI、电信和量子计算之间共享 TFLN 制造基础设施，可能分摊工艺开发和设备成本，不过针对量子级损耗要求的良率需要单独验证。

> 直接的技术好处归于 $XNDU 的光路设计和 HyperLight 的平台，而 $UMC 的收入敞口取决于实际产能分配和产量。

> 这颗 7 mdB 器件的制造地点未披露，因此现有代工合作并不构成对该器件的确认订单。

> 投资者下一步应跟踪封装后的总损耗、目标速度下的消光比、晶圆到晶圆的波动，以及逻辑错误率的改善。

> 数据中心被列为潜在应用；公告中既没有 AI 客户订单，也没有商业系统出货时间表。

原文：- Xanadu ($XNDU) and HyperLight announced 7 mdB insertion loss for a TFLN optical switch on November 10.

- Loss measured in dB fell 65% from the approximately 20 mdB benchmark announced in 2025.

- Converting the reported figures into photon loss gives approximately 0.46% versus 0.16% per switch pass.

- The companies reported high yield and repeatability on HyperLight’s TFLN Chiplet volume wafer manufacturing line.

- The release omitted numerical yield, sample size, switching speed, extinction ratio, and whether packaging loss was included.

- TFLN is thin-film lithium niobate, which uses the Pockels effect to change refractive index with an electric field for fast optical control.

- Xanadu separately reported average fiber-to-chip coupling loss of 0.085 dB per facet.

- HyperLight announced a partnership with $UMC and Wavetek for 6-inch and 8-inch TFLN foundry production in March 2026.

> Quantum information cannot recover from loss through conventional optical amplification, so lower switch loss directly preserves photons reaching the computation.

> Assuming only 100 identical switch passes, calculated transmission rises from 63.1% to 85.1%; this illustration excludes actual system routing depth and all other losses.

> The same switch loss budget could accommodate approximately 2.9 times as many passes, without implying a 2.9-fold increase in logical qubits or computational performance.

> The separately reported 85 mdB coupling loss per facet equals approximately 12 passes through the new switch, keeping packaging and inter-chip connections central to reducing total optical path loss.

> Sharing TFLN manufacturing infrastructure across AI, telecom, and quantum computing could spread process development and equipment costs, although yield against quantum-grade loss requirements needs separate validation.

> The direct technical benefits accrue to $XNDU’s optical path design and HyperLight’s platform, while revenue exposure for $UMC depends on actual production allocation and volume.

> The fabrication site for the 7 mdB device was undisclosed, so the existing foundry partnership does not establish a confirmed order for this device.

> Investors should next track total loss after packaging, extinction ratio at target speed, wafer-to-wafer variation, and improvements in logical error rates.

> Data centers were presented as a potential application; the announcement included neither AI customer orders nor a commercial system shipment schedule.

2026-11-11 04:12 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PhotonCap ｜ 原帖链接

- 北京大学与 SiFotonics 的研究者演示了一个统一的硅光相干平台，覆盖短距和长距光通信。

- 短距光学传统上依赖 VCSEL、EML 和硅光 IM-DD，长距网络则偏好相干传输。

- 研究者开发了一种高功率、窄线宽激光器，将超低损耗 SiN 外腔与 RSOA 结合。

- 该激光器实现了 70 mW 直接输出、亚 1 kHz 线宽，以及 13.7% 的壁插效率。

- 与硅光相干收发器结合，该平台在 256 个波长通道上演示了合计 81.92 Tbps 的带宽。

- 短距传输无需光放大，另一项长距演示超过 4400 公里。

- 波长切换与传输重建在数十微秒内完成。

> PhotonCap 要点

> 关键含义是把相干光学从长距网络延伸到 AI 数据中心。

> SiN 外腔的低相位噪声和高光输出，满足相干收发器的关键激光器要求。

> 短距相干的采用可能扩大 DSP、相干接收器、可调谐激光器和硅光 PIC 的机会。

> 所演示的 81.92 Tbps 是 256 个波长的合计带宽，不是单条光链路。

> 对 AI scale-up 而言，相干 DSP 的功耗、延迟和成本必须变得能与传统 IM-DD 竞争。

> $LITE 和 $COHR 拥有相关激光技术，$MRVL 有相干 DSP 敞口，但该研究并未确立直接的商业利益。

> 关键投资问题是：在哪些传输距离上，相干光学能在每比特能耗和每比特成本上胜过 IM-DD。

原文：- Peking University and SiFotonics researchers demonstrated a unified silicon photonic coherent platform spanning short- and long-distance optical communications.

- Short-reach optics traditionally rely on VCSEL, EML, and SiPh-based IM-DD, while long-haul networks favor coherent transmission.

- The researchers developed a high-power, narrow-linewidth laser combining an ultralow-loss SiN external cavity with an RSOA.

- The laser achieved 70 mW direct output, sub-1 kHz linewidth, and 13.7% wall-plug efficiency.

- Combined with SiPh coherent transceivers, the platform demonstrated 81.92 Tbps aggregate bandwidth across 256 wavelength channels.

- Short-reach transmission required no optical amplification, while a separate long-haul demonstration exceeded 4,400 km.

- Wavelength switching and transmission re-establishment were achieved within tens of microseconds.

> PhotonCap Takeaway

> The key implication is extending coherent optics beyond long-haul networks into AI data centers.

> Low phase noise and high optical output from SiN external cavities address critical laser requirements for coherent transceivers.

> Short-reach coherent adoption could expand opportunities for DSPs, coherent receivers, tunable lasers, and SiPh PICs.

> The demonstrated 81.92 Tbps represents aggregate bandwidth across 256 wavelengths, not a single optical link.

> For AI scale-up, coherent DSP power, latency, and cost must become competitive with conventional IM-DD.

> $LITE and $COHR have relevant laser technologies, while $MRVL has coherent DSP exposure, but the research does not establish direct commercial benefits.

> The critical investment question is where coherent optics can outperform IM-DD in energy per bit and cost per bit across transmission distances.

- EV Group（EVG）宣布，通过先进封装和异构集成，扩大对量子计算的支持。

- 关键技术包括 fusion bonding、hybrid bonding、高真空无氧化物键合、纳米压印光刻，以及薄晶圆处理。

- 对于光子量子计算，EVG 支持将 III-V 族以及高 Pockels 系数材料与光波导进行异构集成。

- Hybrid bonding 可实现高密度自旋量子比特互连，而低温金属键合则支持离子阱 chiplet 集成。

- EVG 于 2026 年 5 月与 Xanadu（$XNDU）合作，开发工业级光子量子硬件制造。

- EVG 还参与 CHAMP-ION，这是一条面向工业级离子阱量子芯片生产的欧洲中试线。

> 量子扩展的瓶颈正在从量子比特数量，扩展到在不牺牲性能的前提下集成异构材料和芯片的制造技术。

> 对于光子量子计算，关键挑战是在保持晶圆级良率的同时，将光源和电光材料与低损耗波导集成在一起。

> EVG 正在把自身定位于 AI CPO 与量子光子学所共享的晶圆键合和异构集成供应链之中。

> 它与 Xanadu 的合作意味着从实验室制备向工业级光子量子制造过渡，不过量产良率和商业规模仍未得到确认。

原文：- EV Group (EVG) announced expanded support for quantum computing through advanced packaging and heterogeneous integration.

- Key technologies include fusion bonding, hybrid bonding, high-vacuum oxide-free bonding, nanoimprint lithography, and thin-wafer processing.

- For photonic quantum computing, EVG supports heterogeneous integration of III-V and high-Pockels-coefficient materials with optical waveguides.

- Hybrid bonding enables dense spin-qubit interconnects, while low-temperature metal bonding supports trapped-ion chiplet integration.

- EVG partnered with Xanadu ($XNDU) in May 2026 to develop industrial-scale photonic quantum hardware manufacturing.

- EVG also participates in CHAMP-ION, a European pilot line for industrial-scale trapped-ion quantum chip production.

> Quantum scaling bottlenecks are expanding beyond qubit counts to manufacturing technologies that integrate heterogeneous materials and chips without compromising performance.

> For photonic quantum computing, the critical challenge is integrating light sources and electro-optic materials with low-loss waveguides while maintaining wafer-level yield.

> EVG is positioning itself within the wafer bonding and heterogeneous integration supply chain shared by AI CPO and quantum photonics.

> Its Xanadu partnership suggests a transition from laboratory fabrication toward industrial-scale photonic quantum manufacturing, although production yields and commercial volumes remain unconfirmed.

2026-11-11 04:24 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@tphuang ｜ 原帖链接

智元机器人发布了其机器人模型 WITA-Omni 的最新版本，声称在所有 Omni 排名体系上达到 SOTA。

最近我看到一些人讨论 Fable 和 Astra 在机器人上有多强。

通用大模型不适用于 AI 机器人，有很多显而易见的原因，但最明显的一条是：模型必须在端侧运行。

因此，你需要更小、聚焦于机器人绝对必需事项的模型，才能高效。它必须小到能放进板载内存。考虑到 KV Cache 的大小，模型规模比你想的要小得多。

通过 WiFi 去调用 Claude 不是选项：反复调用成本过高，再加上确实可能进入信号盲区。我理解 Elon 可能想用星链给 Optimus 机器人和 Grok 推理，但我可以向你保证，纽约有很多建筑里这行不通。

除此之外，专用模型还可以适配该机器人可用的传感器。例如，电子皮肤的特性会成为任何机器人应用的主要输入和训练数据。机器人做的很多事将由触觉传感器决定，而这又取决于手部和身体传感器的位置。这和用视觉传感器数据训练大模型并不一样。

原文：Agibot released latest version of its robotic model WITA-Omni, which it claims to be SOTA across all the Omni ranking systems.

Now, I've seen some ppl talking about how great Fable & Astra are for robotics recently.

There are many obvious reasons why general LLMs don't work for AI robots, but the most obvious one is that you need the model to run on edge.

As such, you need smaller model focused on the things that robot absolutely needs in order to be efficient. It needs to be small enough to be held on the on-board memory. Consider the size of KV Cache, the model sizes are a lot smaller than you'd think.

Wifi to Claude is not an option due to the excessive cost from calling it over and over again + the very real possibility of hitting dead zones. I understand that Elon may want to use Starlink for Optimus robots & Grok inference. But I can assure of many New York buildings where this would not work.

And aside from that, specialized models can cater to the sensors available to that robot. For example, the properties of electronic skin would be major input & training data for any robot applications. Much of what a robot does would be determined by touch sensors, which is again dependent on the hand sensor and body sensors locations. Not exactly the same as using vision sensor data to train your LLMs.（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — 2026 年 11 月 10 日

📊 SMH：$483.63（较前一交易日收盘 -0.78%）

• CoreWeave 的 Q3 营收跳升 168%，至 36.6 亿美元；backlog 达到 1325 亿美元，2026 年营收指引上调至 130–133 亿美元。在运电力超过 1.7GW。需求不是问题：当季净利息费用 8.46 亿美元，远超经营利润 2.07 亿美元，融资经济性仍是焦点。

• 软银在修订美国 AI 数据中心电力设备采购计划（包括其俄亥俄园区）后，确认了 37.3 亿美元亏损。其中包括 28.5 亿美元不可退还的预付款，以及 8.80 亿美元的合同修改与和解。为稀缺发电产能做预定，在项目需求发生变化时会带来真实的下行风险——并非每一笔设备承诺最终都会变成已部署的兆瓦。

• 台积电 10 月营收达到 NT$5868.9 亿，同比增 59.7%，环比增 14.7%。尽管 AI 股票遭到抛售，这家代工厂进入第四季度时仍给出又一个强劲的出货信号。这些是已经实现的销售，而不是客户的支出意向，进一步凸显了半导体经营动能与日益紧张的基础设施投资者之间的落差。

• AMD 已将第六代 EPYC SP7 处理器全面上市，OEM/ODM 系统已在出货，Azure 和 Oracle 云实例即将推出。Zen 6 刷新强化了其 CPU 地位，因 agents 需要协调 GPU 工作负载并运行应用和数据库。AI 基础设施支出并不只是一场加速器竞赛。

• 英飞凌财年第四季度营收 47.1 亿欧元，segment-result 利润率 23.8%。2027 财年指引目标约 200 亿欧元营收，数据中心电源销售接近 40 亿欧元，投资 30–35 亿欧元。功率半导体正在成为与汽车和工业复苏并行的、大得多的增长引擎。

• 微软正在向华盛顿施压，反对限制中国光模块，指出这是 GPU 和电力之外又一个潜在的部署瓶颈。官员们在考虑以 3.2Tbps 为门槛，而不是一刀切切断。Coherent 和 Lumentum 可能从本土化中受益，但在替代供应尚未放量之前就替换中国供应，有可能拖慢美国的安装进度。

• 佳能宣布将于 12 月初推出 FPA-6400ES6a KrF 扫描仪，规格为每小时 400 片晶圆、4nm 套刻精度。该设备还可容纳高达 700 微米的晶圆翘曲。更高通量的光刻对存储和逻辑产能很重要；这是成熟曝光技术上的竞争，而不是 EUV 的替代品。

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 10, 2026

📊 SMH: $483.63 (-0.78% vs prior trading close)

• CoreWeave’s Q3 revenue jumped 168% to $3.66B; backlog reached $132.5B and 2026 revenue guidance rose to $13.0–13.3B. Active power exceeds 1.7GW. Demand is not the problem: $846M of quarterly net interest expense dwarfed $207M of operating income, keeping financing economics firmly in focus.

• SoftBank recognized a $3.73B loss after revising procurement plans for US AI-datacenter power equipment, including its Ohio campus. That comprises $2.85B of non-refundable advances and $880M of contract modifications and settlements. Reserving scarce generation capacity carries real downside when project requirements change—not every equipment commitment becomes deployed megawatts.

• TSMC’s October revenue hit NT$586.89B, up 59.7% year over year and 14.7% sequentially. The foundry enters Q4 with another strong shipment signal despite the AI-stock selloff. These are realized sales, not customer spending intentions, reinforcing the gap between semiconductor operating momentum and increasingly nervous infrastructure investors.

• AMD made sixth-generation EPYC SP7 processors generally available, with OEM/ODM systems shipping and Azure and Oracle cloud instances coming. The Zen 6 refresh strengthens its CPU position as agents coordinate GPU workloads and run applications and databases. AI infrastructure spending is not exclusively an accelerator contest.

• Infineon delivered €4.71B of fiscal Q4 revenue at a 23.8% segment-result margin. FY2027 guidance targets approximately €20B revenue, with datacenter power-supply sales approaching €4B and €3.0–3.5B of investment. Power semiconductors are becoming a much larger growth engine alongside automotive and industrial recovery.

• Microsoft is pressing Washington against restricting Chinese optical transceivers, highlighting another potential deployment bottleneck beyond GPUs and electricity. Officials are considering a 3.2Tbps threshold rather than a sweeping cutoff. Coherent and Lumentum could benefit from localization, but replacing Chinese supply before alternatives scale risks slowing US installations.

• Canon announced its FPA-6400ES6a KrF scanner for early-December launch, specifying 400 wafers per hour and 4nm overlay accuracy. The tool also accommodates wafer warpage up to 700 microns. Higher-throughput lithography matters for memory and logic capacity; this is competition in mature exposure technology, not an EUV replacement.

Marcelo 我喜欢你，但为什么人们因为 Anthropic 在三天内让模型的一部分在 AMD 上跑起来，就不断说“CUDA is dead”？

这确实了不起。但据此宣布 NVIDIA 的护城河已经消失，就是胡扯。

CUDA 不是你移植过去的几行代码。它是一个庞大的库、工具生态系统，以及多年的优化，开发者将其用于 AI、科学、机器人等领域。

正如 John Linford 和我在视频中讨论的（链接见下），让某个东西在另一块芯片上跑起来是一回事。让整个工作负载在规模上可靠运行，并具备有竞争力的性能和成本，则是另一回事。

竞争是真实存在的，AI 也会让使用其他硬件变得更容易。但这并不意味着 NVIDIA 数十年来打造的整个生态系统就这样消失了。

原文：Love you Marcelo but why do people keep saying “CUDA is dead” because Anthropic got part of a model running on AMD in three days.

That’s impressive. But declaring NVIDIA’s moat gone from that is bullshit.

CUDA isn’t just a few lines of code you port over. It’s a massive ecosystem of libraries, tools and years of optimization that developers use across AI, science, robotics and more.

As John Linford and I discussed in our video (link below) getting something to run on another chip is one thing. Getting the whole workload running reliably at scale, with competitive performance and cost, is another.

Competition is real, and AI will make it easier to use other hardware. That doesn’t mean the entire ecosystem NVIDIA built over decades just disappeared.

@demian_ai（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 9 条）

2026-11-11 05:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @demian_ai ｜ 原帖链接

人们曾经说：“计算机时代到处都能看见，唯独看不见在生产率统计里。”

工厂用上电动机大约 40 年后，才真正变得更快。

第一台大约在 1884 年进入工厂。

到 1899 年，电动机还运转着美国工厂里不到 5% 的机器，直到大约 1919 年才超过一半。

即便那时，多数厂主做的仍是最显而易见的事：把地下室里的大型蒸汽机拆掉，在原来的位置放上一台大电动机，其他一切照旧。

一台电机带动天花板上的一根长铁轴，皮带从上面落到地板上的每台机器，整个系统从早转到晚，不管你开的是一台车床还是五十台。

好处是在人们停止复制旧布局之后才出现的。当每台机器有了自己的小电机，你不再需要那些轴，也就不再需要围着动力源堆起来的又高又窄的厂房。

工厂变成平坦的单层，机器按工序流动的顺序排列，一根皮带断裂也不再让整间屋子停工。

单位资本的动力在 30 年里几乎没变，然后在 1920 和 30 年代快速上升，也正是工厂生产率起飞的那些年。

斯坦福经济学家保罗·戴维在 1990 年用这个故事，回答罗伯特·索洛那句“计算机时代到处可见、生产率统计里却看不见”。

计算机带来的好处，几年后、在 90 年代末确实出现了。

所以当人们争论这类图表、争论 AI 是否已经开始兑现时，我会看谁在拆除那根天轴，也就是谁在推倒重来。

大多数公司仍只是把模型螺栓固定在旧的组织架构上，但我的猜测是：当有人围绕它建成“单层厂房”版的公司时，数字才会动起来。

（所引用 @GavinSBaker：认为 FT 的图表、尤其第一张，已能看到一些“AI 的 ROI”证据。）

原文：People used to say « you could see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics »

Factories had electric motors for about 40 years before they got much faster

the first one went into a factory around 1884.

by 1899 motors ran less than 5% of the machinery in american factories, and they didn't pass half until about 1919.

even then most owners did the obvious thing. they pulled the big steam engine out of the basement, put a big electric motor where it used to sit, and kept everything else.

one motor turned a long iron shaft along the ceiling, belts dropped from it to every machine on the floor, and the whole thing spun from morning to night whether you were running one lathe or fifty.

the gains showed up once people stopped copying the old layout. when every machine got its own small motor, you didn't need the shafts, so you didn't need tall narrow buildings stacked around the power source.

Factories went flat and single story, machines got lined up in the order the work moved, and one snapped belt no longer stopped a whole room.

Power per unit of capital stayed flat for 30 years, then climbed fast through the 1920s and 30s, the same years factory productivity took off.

paul david, the stanford economist, used this in 1990 to answer robert solow's line that you could see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics.

The computer gains did show up a few years later, in the late 90s.

So when people argue over charts like these about whether ai is paying off yet, i'd watch who tears out the line shaft aka rebuilds from scratch

most companies are still bolting a model onto the old org chart but my guess is the numbers move when somebody builds the single story version of a company around it引用原帖（@GavinSBaker）：Hard for me to look at these charts from the FT, especially the first one, and not see some evidence of “ROI on AI.”

Seems like a productivity surge post-Covid era overhiring that began to reverse before ChatGPT came out and then resumed its upward trend after ChatGPT.

Especially given the @a16z data on revenue per employee at their portfolio companies and the @EPBResearch data showing that the… 链接

2026-11-10 23:27 北京时间 ｜ Source: @demian_ai ｜ 原帖链接

这很有意思。

在 1870 年代，一份中文电报比英文电报更贵，而今天的大模型对很多语言做了类似的事。

摩斯电码是围绕英语建的。“E”作为最常见字母，只是一个点。

中文有数千个字、没有字母表，所以 1871 年的中文电报码给每个字一个四位数，而每个数字要花五个点划——摩斯电码里最长的符号。一个字要发出二十个信号。

于是一条 25 个词的电报，中文编码大约要半小时，英文大约只要两分钟。

大模型按 token 计费，也就是分词器把文本切成的块。

分词器主要从英文和拉丁字母网页文本里学习切块，于是其他语言被切得更碎，要为更多块付钱。

即便在网上最大的 20 种语言里，最贵的也需要最便宜那种的 1.5 到 5 倍 token；在 200 种语言上，某一模型的差距达到 17 倍。

更多 token 还意味着上下文窗口能装下的更少，回答也更慢。文字系统本身比语言有多罕见伤害更大：缅甸文、藏文、高棉文和老挝文最吃亏。

每张网络都按建造者所用的单位定价，用其他单位写作的人就要一直付这个差价，直到有人重新设计这套编码。

原文：this is fascinating

in the 1870s a chinese telegram cost more than an english one, and LLMs do a version of the same thing to a lot of languages today

morse code was built around english.
"E", the most common letter, is a single dot.

Chinese has thousands of characters and no alphabet, so the 1871 chinese telegraph code gave each character a four digit number, and every digit costs five dots and dashes, the longest symbols in morse. One character went out as twenty signals.

-> so a 25 word message took about half an hour to encode, against about two minutes in english.

LLMs bill by the token, the chunks a tokenizer chops your text into.

Tokenizers learned their chunks mostly from english and latin script web text, so other languages get chopped finer and pay for more pieces.

even among the 20 biggest languages online, the priciest needs 1.5 to 5 times the tokens of the cheapest, and across 200 languages one model's gap reached 17x.

More tokens also means less fits in the context window and answers come back slower. The script hurts more than how rare the language is: burmese, tibetan, khmer and lao come off worst. ​

Every network gets priced in the units of whoever built it, and everyone writing in other units pays the difference until somebody redesigns the code.

因此，先进失效分析的关键价值，并不只是把异常检测出来。

而是缩短从失效检测到定位根因并采取纠正措施所需的时间。

对先进封装制造商而言，这种能力可以直接影响产品开发进度、良率爬升和制造可靠性。

原文：The critical value of advanced failure analysis is therefore not simply detecting abnormalities.

It is reducing the time required to move from failure detection to root-cause identification and corrective action.

For advanced packaging manufacturers, this capability can directly affect product development schedules, yield ramp-up, and manufacturing reliability.

Gemini 4 Carbon 今日开始推送。它为持续、长周期任务而建，能在复杂的软件工程、网络安全和科学发现中进行深入推理。

将通过 Gemini API 在 @GoogleAIStudio 和企业版 Gemini 中全球提供，并面向 Ultra 订阅用户在 @antigravity、@GeminiApp 以及搜索中的 AI Mode 推出。

原文：Gemini 4 Carbon is rolling out today. It’s built for sustained, long-horizon tasks, reasoning deeply through complex software engineering, cybersecurity, and scientific discovery.

Coming globally via the Gemini API in @GoogleAIStudio and Gemini for business, and for Ultra subscribers in @antigravity, the @GeminiApp, and AI Mode in Search.

2026-11-11 01:28 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

我们的团队把 Gemini 4 Carbon 用在一项重大安全挑战上：对 Signal 协议底层代码进行数学证明。

借助 AlphaProof Code，@GoogleDeepMind 研究人员引导代理把该协议验证到已编译的设备代码，补上真实漏洞藏身的缝隙。结果是：

- Gemini 4 Carbon 生成了超过 600 万行经机器检查的 Lean 证明
- 数年专家手工验证被压缩到只需要数周
- 约 1% 的代码库需要人工审阅（其余由 Lean 自动检查）

这正帮助解锁可扩展的形式化验证，用于构建安全软件，为从根本上把优势转向防御者奠定基础。我们将向安全社区开源这些结果。

原文：Our teams put Gemini 4 Carbon to work on a major security challenge: mathematically proving the code underlying the Signal protocol.

Using AlphaProof Code, @GoogleDeepMind researchers guided agents to verify the protocol down to compiled device code, closing the gap where real bugs hide. The result:

- Gemini 4 Carbon generated over 6M lines of machine-checked Lean proofs
- years of expert manual verification compressed to just weeks
~1% of the codebase required human review (Lean automatically checked the rest)

This is helping unlock scalable formal verification for building secure software, laying the groundwork to fundamentally shift the advantage to defenders. We’re open sourcing the results for the security community.

代理未来与企业文件打交道的次数，将是今天人类的 100 倍。正因如此，我们很高兴与 Anthropic 合作，通过 Claude 为 Box 内容带来完整的读写互操作。

现在，代理不仅可以在工作流中调取内容来处理，还可以通过 MCP 把文件更新写回 Box，并直接在 Claude 内查看。你可以创建、编辑和更新合同、演示文稿、战略文档以及任何其他内容。

我们创办 Box 时，无法想象未来会以所有这些方式处理非结构化数据，但很清楚的是，代理和人都将产出远超我们想象的工件。我们正在打造的平台，让你可以极为顺畅且安全地，从任何代理处理你的内容。

原文：Agents are going to work with enterprise files 100X more than people do today. This is why we're excited to partner with Anthropic to bring full read/write interoperability to Box content via Claude.

Now you can have an agent not only pull up content to work with in a workflow, but also write back file updates to Box and view them directly inside of Claude, all via MCP. You can create, edit, and update contracts, presentations, strategy docs, and any other content.

When we started Box we couldn't have imagined all the ways that we'd be working with our unstructured data in the future, but it's clear that agents and people will be producing vastly more artifacts than we imagined. 2026-11-11 13:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@levie ｜ 原帖链接

Agent swarm（智能体集群）有潜力再一次推动 AI 产出跃升。影响将是：采用这种工作模式的团队，与不采用的团队之间，会出现清晰的分化。

大致的演进是：聊天给了我们快速的 AI 回合响应，让我们能快一点，但我们仍受限于交互的速率；然后是可以离开去替我们完成整项任务的 agent，把数小时或数天的工作压缩到几分钟；再到现在的 agent swarm，它们会主动追寻目标，并找到任何路径把项目成功做完，生产力提升没有内在上限。

可以想象这样一个未来：凡是能在一个自包含的数字环境里创造价值的任务，swarm 都能比任何其他方式更快完成。

本质上，凡是价值创造目前受限于你能做多少研究、能用一套软件工具做多少决策、能处理或消费多少数据、以及能生成多少代码（或类代码产物）的领域，都适用。

这意味着 swarm 将在游戏开发、编程、交易、生命科学研究、IT 自动化、网络安全、数学突破、动画，以及其他具备这些特征的工作领域扮演重要角色。

围绕这些 agent 如何运转，去打造 harness、应用型 AI 产品和基础设施，机会很多。最重要的是，能把工作以这种方式串起来的人，机会巨大。这将是一个全新的 FDE 工作领域。

原文：Agent swarms have the potential to drive yet another leap in output from AI. The impact will be a clear separation between teams that adopt this pattern of work and those that don’t.

The progression has roughly been: chat gave us quick turn AI responses that let us work a bit faster, but we were still rate limited by our interactions; then agents that can go off and complete entire tasks for us, accelerating hours or days of work into minutes; to now swarms of agents that are goal seeking and will find any route to the successful completion of a project, where there’s no inherent limit to the productivity gains.

We can imagine a future where, for any task in which value can be created within a self-contained digital environment, swarms will be able to complete the work faster than any other approach.

Essentially any area where value creation is currently bound by the amount of research you can do, the amount of decisions made with a set of software tools, the amount of data processed or consumed, and the amount of code (or code adjacent) that can be generated.

This means swarms will play a big role in game development, coding, trading, life sciences research, IT automation, cyber, math breakthroughs, animation, and plenty of other areas of work that have these characteristics.

There’s a lot of opportunity to build the harnesses, applied AI products, and infrastructure for how these agents operate. And most importantly there’s a ton of opportunity for those that can wire up work in this way. It’s going to be an entire new field of FDE work available.引用原帖（@?）：Swarm riders and Swarm-native companies

AI-use even among highly AI-native people is power-law distributed.

There seems to be a new class of people — call them “swarm riders” — who are capable of consistently commanding large swarms of agents to productive ends.

This is a new kind of managerial skill, analogous to the skill it takes to manage large organizations of people, but wholly different … 链接

But I think this is super interesting evidence about what the true major source of algorithmic progress is. https://twitter.com/dwarkesh_sp/status/2097359573581799481引用原帖（@dwarkesh_sp）：Pretraining progress seems to be coming mostly from data improvements.

@who_is_jerbear and I pretrained combinations of year-representative open model recipes and data corpuses across 2019 to 2025 at various small scales.

Data improvements contributed 3.24x as many compute multipliers as model improvements did (12.0x vs 3.7x).

And the gains stack independently - a better dataset helps every arc… 链接

@PatrickToulme（保留 3 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 5 条）

2026-11-10 23:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PatrickToulme ｜ 原帖链接

AI 进步的最新“牺牲品”是 AI 框架本身。还有谁记得大家都在争论 JAX 对 PyTorch 的时候？这场争论现在基本上没意义了。

xAI 大概在这里用了一个 RSI 循环，用 ISA / megakernel 生成了全部内核。我估计可能是几千个代理组成的 swarm / 嵌套树 swarm 工作了一周。他们还可以让这个 RSI 循环 24/7 运行，持续改进技术栈。

用在推理上相当简单，因为你只需要担心前向传播的数值。用在 RL 上肯定更难，但仍可管理。这里大部分工作是为这个 RSI 循环建好验证环。如果你有一个非常好的验证环，swarm 就会收敛到一个很好的解。

（所引用 @elonmusk：已弃用 JAX/SGLang，用 C/C++ 从零重写整个 RL 和推理栈；预训练已完成，Grok 4.8 正在 RL，本周稍后将向 Bot 用户软推出。）

原文：The latest casualty from AI progress is the AI framework itself. Who remembers when everyone would debate JAX vs PyTorch? That debate is pretty moot at this point.

xAI probably used an RSI loop here to generate all the kernels in ISA / megakernel. Probably a few thousand agents swarm / nested tree swarms working for a week IMO. They can also keep this RSI loop running 24/7 improving their stack.

Doing this for inference is pretty trivial, as you only have to worry about forward pass numerics. Doing this for RL is definitely harder, but still manageable. Most of the work here is setting up the verification loop for the RSI loop here. If you have a very good verification loop, then the swarm will converge on a very good solution.引用原帖（@elonmusk）：We have dumped JAX/SGLang and rewritten the entire RL and inference stack from scratch in C/C++, which took some extra time.

Training (aka pre-training) is done. Lot of cleanup was needed on the SFT data to get that mostly right. Now, 4.8 is in RL and the evals are starting to climb. We will do a soft rollout to Bot users later this week and see how it goes.

Grok 4.8 will bear no resemblance to… 链接

2026-11-10 23:40 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PatrickToulme ｜ 原帖链接

我可以谈谈 XLA GPU 这一块。
如果你用的是 XLA GPU 代码生成，也就是不自己写内核和通信——那么是的，Elon 的新栈会全天候胜过它。

但是——如果你用 PTX 自己写全部内核，把它们封装成 XLA 里的 custom_calls，然后由 XLA 把整个程序 CudaGraph 起来，这时已经没有代码生成、只是你的 PTX 内核——你会接近 @elonmusk 的 C 栈。我仍然认为他的 C 栈更快。

原文：I can talk a bit about the XLA GPU part.
If you are using XLA GPU codegeneration meaning not writing your own kernels and comms - then yes Elon's new stack is gonna beat it all day.

But - if you write all your own kernels in say PTX, wrap those as custom_calls in XLA and then XLA will CudaGraph the entire program which is now just your PTX kernels with 0 codegeneration - you will get close to @elonmusk C stack. I do still think his C stack is faster IMO.所回复原帖（@zeeshanp_）：i wonder how much of an improvement swapping to the c/c++ stack brought in terms of tokens/s/gpu and how fast each pretraining run can be done.

if you use jax, at some point you realize you need to rip out xla and rewrite the comms schedule and compute kernels from scratch anyways if you want very high training throughput. 链接

Claude Desktop：
+ 终于流畅得像黄油一样。每个 UI 元素都很快。Fork 是瞬时的！谢天谢地
+ Claude iPhone 应用终于有了扎实的远程会话
+ 我其实更喜欢点一下线程上的小圆点，再按键盘字母来 fork/pin/archive。ChatGPT Desktop 用的是那些荒谬的三键快捷键。
+ Auto repair 非常好用。比起 Codex，更有可能把一个 PR 从头跟到尾
+ 从 fork 出来的子线程 UI 很好
+ iPhone 应用上在工作和个人账户之间快速切换，很快！
+ 后续建议的 chips/cards 太棒了。“我在你的代码里发现了这个难看的怪物，要不要我再开一个线程把它清理掉？”要要要。
+ rewind 按钮的 UX 比 Codex 的 edit 按钮更好
- 我仍然很难判断一个线程是否正在运行。那个浅浅的渐变扫过，远没有 Codex 的 spinner 显眼。

ChatGPT Desktop：
+ 仍然是我的主力
+ 很难打败 6.1 Sol 的性价比（intelligence per price）
+ 应用重启后还能接续正在进行的线程，体验非常好
+ Projects 是很好的组织工具
- iPhone 应用仍然没有快速账户切换！你们落后了，快上线吧！
- 我看到你们在试图把它做成一个全能（omni）应用，但 URL 默认不在我的浏览器里打开，其实非常烦人。还要点右键，真是累赘。我其实不希望网页在一个窄窄的竖直侧栏里打开。
- 我从来没有故意点过 “start side chat” 或 “add to chat” 按钮

原文：It’s time for my quarterly coding UX review. For the first time Claude may actually be leading:

Claude Desktop:
+ It’s finally buttery smooth. Every UI element is just fast. Forks are instant! Hallelujah
+ Claude iPhone app finally has solid remote sessions
+ I actually prefer the click dots on thread -> press keyboard letter to fork/pin/archive. chatGPT desktop has these absurd three key shortcuts.
+ Auto repair is really nice. Much more likely to see a PR through end to end than codex
+ The sub-threads UI from forks is nice
+ Fast account switching between work and personal accounts on iPhone app!
+ The follow-up chips/cards are wonderful. “I found this ugly monster lurking in your code, want me to spin up another thread to clean it up?” Yes yes yes.
+ The rewind button is nicer UX than codex’s edit button
- Still hard for me to tell if a thread is currently running. The light gradient pass is much less visible than codex’s spinner.

ChatGPT Desktop:
+ Still my primary workhorse
+ Hard to beat the intelligence per price of 6.1 Sol
+ The continuation of ongoing threads between app restarts is so nice
+ Projects are nice organizational tools
- Still no fast account switching on the iPhone app! You’re behind here, ship it!
- I see you trying to turn it into an omni app but it’s actually really annoying that URLs don’t default open in my browser. The right click is a nuisance. I actually don’t want my webpages opening in a tiny vertical side pane.
- I have never intentionally clicked the “start side chat” button or the “add to chat” button

@kimmonismus（保留 30 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 54 条）

2026-11-10 22:23 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

这对 Fable 5.2 是一次非常重要的胜利。它在衡量真实世界工作的最重要基准之一上，取得了目前最显著的跃升。

在 Remote Labor Index 上，它完成了 41.3% 的项目，至少达到了人类原作的标准。Fable 5.1 的得分是 17.9%。成功完成的项目数是此前的两倍以上。

2026-11-11 04:21 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

让我简要总结一下 AI 现在走到了哪里。

每个人都能感觉到，也都能看到。我相信 2026 年是转折点。我们每天都看到重大进展，但在对最新基准的兴奋中，很容易忽略更大的图景。

Anthropic 刚刚发布了其最新旗舰模型 Claude Fable 5.2。它非常出色。任何测试过的人都知道它有多好；在博客中，Anthropic 也强调了编码之外的进展：物理学和硬件设计中的新发现，以及与 Mythos 5.2 一起开发的预测病毒蛋白结构的方法。

Fable 5.2 甚至通过在八天里协调超过 1000 个子代理，做出了一个操作系统原型。与此同时，Anthropic 在系统卡中承认，它有一个单独的、未发布的研究模型，能力略高于 Mythos 5.2。这可能是“Model 4”，尽管这一联系尚未得到确认。

与此同时，竞争并未放缓。Google 已经大幅追上，Gemini 4 Carbon 非常出色。Google 还表示正用自己的模型做研究并取得新发现。有传闻称一个名为 Krypton 的后继者已在测试，但其能力仍未确认。

今天，AI 2027 的作者更新了预测。AI Futures Project 现在预计：除非出现重大放缓，能够胜任顶尖人类 AI 研究团队工作的 AI 很可能在 2027 年到来。他们估计，AI 驱动的 AI 研究加速，已经比他们原先情景提前了五个月。

我们也不应忘记 AI 系统已经在数学上取得的成就。OpenAI 称一个内部系统给出了 Navier–Stokes 千禧年奖问题的一个解。而按 OpenAI 博客所说，这个模型已经过时了。

我想说的是：我经常说起飞始于 2026 年，2027 年将是疯狂的一年。也许是人类历史上最疯狂的一年。但当我们回看今年，我认为会看清楚 2026 年已经有多疯狂。

我从 GPT-3.5 起就投身 AI，现在全职工作，为的是让这场技术革命可被感知。就在几年前，像 GPT-4 这样的大模型还显得像随机鹦鹉。它们会幻觉，和今天的模型相比，感觉像平凡的机器。当然，它们从来不是真的平凡。但回看，自 2024 年底 o1 以来的发展速度一直在加快，现在能力的突然爆发已能被切实感受到。

所以请享受相对平静的最后几个月。我相信 2027 年将是超级智能之年。之后一切都不会再一样。

原文：Let me briefly sum up where we are with AI.

Everyone can feel it and everyone can see it. I believe 2026 is the turning point. We see major advances every day, but with all the excitement about the latest benchmarks, it’s easy to lose sight of the bigger picture.

Anthropic has just released Claude Fable 5.2, its latest flagship model. It’s outstanding. Anyone who has tested it knows how good it is, and in its blog posts, Anthropic has also highlighted advances beyond coding: new findings in physics and hardware design, as well as a method for predicting viral protein structures developed with Mythos 5.2.

Fable 5.2 even built an operating system prototype by coordinating more than 1,000 subagents over eight days. At the same time, Anthropic acknowledged in its system card that it has a separate, unreleased research model that is somewhat more capable than Mythos 5.2. This could be “Model 4,” although that connection has not been confirmed.

Meanwhile, the competition isn’t slowing down. Google has caught up in a big way, and Gemini 4 Carbon is outstanding. Google also says it is using its own models for research and making new discoveries. Rumors suggest that a successor called Krypton is already being tested, though its capabilities remain unconfirmed.

Today, the authors of AI 2027 updated their prediction. The AI Futures Project now forecasts that AI capable of doing the work of a team of top human AI researchers will likely arrive in 2027, absent a major slowdown. They estimate that AI-driven acceleration of AI research is already five months ahead of their original scenario.

And we shouldn’t forget what AI systems have already achieved in mathematics. OpenAI says an internal system produced a solution to the Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem. And this model is already outdated, as OpenAI said in their blogpost.

What I’m trying to say is this: I regularly say that the takeoff began in 2026 and that 2027 will be a crazy year. Perhaps the craziest year in human history. But when we look back on this year, I think it will become clear just how crazy 2026 already was.

I’ve devoted myself to AI since GPT-3.5, and I now work full-time to make this technological revolution tangible. Just a few years ago, LLMs like GPT-4 seemed like stochastic parrots. They hallucinated and, compared with today’s models, felt like trivial machines. Of course, they were never actually trivial. But looking back, the pace of development since o1 in late 2024 has kept increasing, and now the sudden explosion in capabilities feels tangible.

So enjoy these last months of relative calm. I believe 2027 will be the year of superintelligence. And nothing will be the same again.

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/1 Physical Intelligence 的 π0.5，在加入一个很小的触觉编码器后，在 26 项真实世界机器人任务上的成功率从 48% 升至 70%。

该编码器是来自 @robbyant_brain 的 LingBot-Touch（蚂蚁集团旗下的具身智能公司），并且是开源的。Tiny 版本有 546 万参数，不到它所接入策略的 0.2%。

原文：/1 Physical Intelligence's π0.5 goes from 48% to 70% success on 26 real-world robot tasks once you add a small touch encoder.

That encoder is LingBot-Touch from @robbyant_brain, an embodied AI company under Ant Group, and it's open source. The Tiny version has 5.46M parameters, under 0.2% of the policy it plugs into.

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/2 有意思的是与原始传感器数据的比较。把同样的原始触觉信号喂给这些策略，最多只能获得大约一半的增益。

在四种策略（Diffusion Policy、π0.5、LingBot-VLA、T-Rex）上，学到的触觉 token 增加了 14 到 25 个百分点。按论文，该设置在 104 个任务-策略组合中的 89 个上效果最好。

原文：/2 What's interesting is the comparison with raw sensor data. Feeding the same policies the raw touch signals gave at most about half the gain.

Across four policies (Diffusion Policy, π0.5, LingBot-VLA, T-Rex), the learned touch tokens added 14 to 25 percentage points. Per the paper, that setup came out best in 89 of 104 task-policy pairs.所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：/1 Physical Intelligence's π0.5 goes from 48% to 70% success on 26 real-world robot tasks once you add a small touch encoder.

That encoder is LingBot-Touch from @robbyant_brain, an embodied AI company under Ant Group, and it's open source. The Tiny version has 5.46M parameters, under 0.2% of the policy it plugs into. 链接

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/3 训练数据是你无法从网上爬到的那部分。Robbyant 记录了大约 5000 小时的触觉、13.3 亿帧，来自戴着触觉手套的人和遥操作的机器人手。

没有人会上传自己握杯子有多用力、或它开始滑动的那一刻。必须有人付钱去记录，这使它成为眼下机器人领域最稀缺的输入之一。

原文：/3 The training data is the part you can't scrape. Robbyant recorded about 5,000 hours of touch, 1.33 billion frames, from people wearing tactile gloves and from teleoperated robot hands.

Nobody uploads how hard they gripped a cup or the moment it started to slip. Someone has to pay to record it, and that makes it one of the scarcest inputs in robotics right now.所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：/2 What's interesting is the comparison with raw sensor data. Feeding the same policies the raw touch signals gave at most about half the gain.

Across four policies (Diffusion Policy, π0.5, LingBot-VLA, T-Rex), the learned touch tokens added 14 to 25 percentage points. Per the paper, that setup came out best in 89 of 104 task-policy pairs. 链接

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/4 一个编码器读取三种非常不同的传感器类型：磁式、视触觉和压阻式。

我正在重读 AI2027 作者的博客文章。令人震惊的是，有那么多人忽略了其中的关键要点。

这不只是 AI 发展快于预期，而是按作者的说法，这意味着什么：

- “超人类 AI 研究者”（Superhuman AI Researcher，SAR）很可能在 2027 年的某个时点出现。其后果是：“我们还预测，有 35–65% 的概率，‘主导顶级专家的 AI’（Top-Expert-Dominating AI，TED-AI）——即能够自动化全部白领工作的 AI——将在 2028 年之前到来。”

- 此外，他们假设中国将窃取相关底层机密，从而导致美国失去主导地位：“另外，如果我们在不久的将来训练 SAR，中国很可能能够窃取训练配方，甚至可能窃取模型权重。”

- 他们估计，AI 已经把总体进步加速了 1.7 倍，这一效应远比只是让单个员工的生产率提高 1.7 倍更重要。这一节奏，即便是他们最激进的预测，在三个月前赋予的概率也不足 4%。换句话说，即便是他们最乐观的假设，也已经被超越了。

原文：I am currently re-reading the blog post by the authors of AI2027. It is shocking how many people have overlooked the essential points.

It is not just about AI developing faster than anticipated; it is about what that implies, according to the authors:

- A "Superhuman AI Researcher" (SAR) will likely emerge sometime in 2027. The consequence: "We also predict a 35–65% chance that 'Top-Expert-Dominating AI' (TED-AI)—AIs capable of automating all white-collar work—will arrive before 2028."

- Furthermore, they assume that China will steal the underlying secrets, leading to the loss of U.S. dominance: "Additionally, if we train SAR in the near future, China will probably be able to steal the training recipe and maybe even the model weights."

- They estimate that AI is already accelerating overall progress by a factor of 1.7, an effect far more significant than simply making individual employees 1.7 times more productive. This is a pace to which even their most aggressive forecast had assigned a probability of less than 4% just three months ago. In other words, even their most optimistic assumptions have been surpassed.引用原帖（@kimmonismus）：Let me briefly sum up where we are with AI.

Everyone can feel it and everyone can see it. I believe 2026 is the turning point. We see major advances every day, but with all the excitement about the latest benchmarks, it’s easy to lose sight of the bigger picture.

2026-11-11 02:22 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

我们与 Google DeepMind 合作，推出他们的 Multimodal Knowledge Agent（MKA）评测，这是一个面向复杂视觉文件上的代理知识工作的新基准。

@GoogleDeepMind 的 MKA 要求代理审阅视觉上复杂的项目文件，如原理图和扫描件，并产出幻灯片、电子表格、报告和标注图像等交付物。

Multimodal Knowledge Agent（MKA）概览：

➤ 跨 10 个行业的 200 项私有真实世界视觉任务：代理从密集的视觉项目文件出发，包括建筑图纸、工程原理图和临床扫描，产出包括幻灯片、电子表格、PDF、Word 文档和标注图像。与许多代理评测不同，这些输入强调真实的视觉复杂性和领域特殊性，并且是完成任务所真正需要的，而不仅仅是额外上下文。

➤ Climb vs. Regression 量规评分：每项任务按专家量规打分，平均每项约 70 个加权标准。标准分为“Regression”（模型应当通过的基线检查）和“Climb”（用于区分强模型的更难检查）。头条分数是 Climb WSR（加权满足率）——成功完成的 Climb 量规标准的加权比例。

➤ 在 Stirrup 中用代理评委评估：我们在开源代理 harness Stirrup 中运行每项任务，配备代码执行、网页搜索、网页抓取和看图工具（最多 250 轮），并用 GPT-6 Luna（medium）作为代理评委给提交的交付物打分。

关键 MKA 结果：

➤ Claude Fable 5.2 在多模态知识工作上领先：Anthropic 新近发布的 Claude Fable 5.2（max）以 57% 的 Climb WSR 领跑 MKA 榜，随后是 Gemini 4 Carbon（high）的 54%，以及 Claude Opus 5.5（max）的 53%。

➤ 在距最高分 10 分以内，每项任务成本相差近 30 倍：Claude Fable 5.2 平均每项任务 31.97 美元，比 Gemini 4 Carbon（6.29 美元）贵 5 倍以上，却只多约 2 分；比 GPT-6.1 Sol（1.11 美元）贵约 29 倍。在 Carbon 之下，所测试的四个 OpenAI 模型（GPT-6 Astra、GPT-6.1 Sol、GPT-6.1 Terra 和 GPT-6 Luna）都位于成本-性能帕累托前沿，Claude Haiku 5.5 和 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 构成前沿的其余部分。

➤ 没有单一模型在全部 10 个行业垂直领域领先：Claude Fable 5.2 领跑五个领域（软件与数据、工程、物流与测绘、商业，以及与 Claude Sonnet 5.5 并列的金融），Claude Opus 5.5（max）领跑教育和建筑，Gemini 4 Argon（high）领跑科学，Gemini 4 Carbon（high）领跑医疗，Claude Sonnet 5.5（max）以微弱优势领跑法律与政府。

感谢 @GoogleDeepMind 团队构建该基准并与我们合作，将其独家带到 Artificial Analysis！

原文：We’ve partnered with Google DeepMind to launch their Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) evaluation, a new benchmark for agentic knowledge work over complex visual files

@GoogleDeepMind’s MKA is a new benchmark requiring agents to review visually complex project files such as schematics and scans, and produce deliverables like slide decks, spreadsheets, reports, and marked-up images

Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) overview:

➤ 200 private real-world visual tasks across 10 industries: Agents work from dense visual project files including architectural drawings, engineering schematics, and clinical scans to produce deliverables including slide decks, spreadsheets, PDFs, Word documents, and marked-up images. Unlike many agentic evaluations, these inputs emphasize realistic visual complexity and domain-specificity, and are genuinely required to complete the task rather than just providing additional context

➤ Climb vs. Regression rubric scoring: Each task is graded against expert rubrics, averaging ~70 weighted criteria per task. Criteria are split into ‘Regression’ (baseline checks models should pass) and ‘Climb’ criteria (harder checks designed to separate strong models). The headline score is Climb WSR (Weighted Satisfaction Rate) - the weighted proportion Climb rubric criteria successfully completed

➤ Evaluated in Stirrup with an agentic judge: We run every task in Stirrup, our open source agent harness, with code execution, web search, web fetch, and image-viewing tools (up to 250 turns), and grade submitted deliverables using GPT-6 Luna (medium) as an agentic judge

Key MKA results:

➤ Claude Fable 5.2 leads on multimodal knowledge work: Anthropic’s recently released Claude Fable 5.2 (max) leads the MKA leaderboard with a Climb WSR of 57%, followed by Gemini 4 Carbon (high) at 54% and Claude Opus 5.5 (max) at 53%

➤ Cost per Task spans nearly 30× within 10 points of the top score: Claude Fable 5.2 averages $31.97 per task, over 5× Gemini 4 Carbon ($6.29) for ~2 extra points, and ~29× GPT-6.1 Sol ($1.11). Below Carbon, all four OpenAI models tested sit on the cost-performance Pareto frontier (GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6.1 Terra, and GPT-6 Luna), with Claude Haiku 5.5 and MiMo-V2.6-Pro making up the rest of the frontier

➤ No single model leads across all 10 industry verticals: Claude Fable 5.2 leads five verticals (Software & Data, Engineering, Logistics & Mapping, Business, and Finance alongside Claude Sonnet 5.5), Claude Opus 5.5 (max) leads Education and Construction, Gemini 4 Argon (high) leads Science, Gemini 4 Carbon (high) leads Healthcare, and Claude Sonnet 5.5 (max) narrowly leads Law & Government

Thank you to the @GoogleDeepMind team for their work building this benchmark and collaborating with us to bring it exclusively to Artificial Analysis!

Keenable Search 1.0 在 Artificial Analysis Search Index 上获得第 1（pro，83）和第 2（realtime，82）：领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）和 Perplexity（medium）的 80 分。

我们在 Stirrup 代理 harness 中，把 @KeenableAI 的 Keenable Search 1.0 的两种模式（pro 和 realtime）作为 web_search 工具，完成了 1700 项以搜索为重点的任务。

按每 1000 次搜索 4 美元计，Keenable 两种模式每项任务成本约 0.040 美元，比榜单中位数（0.063 美元）低三分之一以上。它也是我们测量过的最快产品之一：每项任务 15.0 秒（realtime）和 15.5 秒（pro），分列榜上第 2、第 3。

Keenable Search 1.0 的关键评测结果：

➤ Artificial Analysis Search Index：Keenable（pro）83 分、Keenable（realtime）82 分，较我们 8 月首次评测时两种模式的 67 分上升，也比同一模型在无搜索时（33 分）高出约 50 分。两者都领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）和 Perplexity（medium）的 80 分。

➤ DeepSearchQA 是 Keenable 最强的基准：两种模式 F1 均为 88，在 26 个产品中排第 1、第 2，领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）的 86。Pro 还在 BrowseComp 上并列第 1（87%，与 Perplexity medium 持平），在 AA-Omniscience 上亦并列第 1（73%，与 Exa auto dynamic highlights 和 Firecrawl 持平）。

➤ 快速搜索：realtime 每项任务的搜索时间 2.3 秒（26 个中第 2），pro 为 3.0 秒（第 3），榜单中位数为 12.5 秒。每次搜索平均约 330ms（realtime）和 390ms（pro）返回。

关键细节：

➤ 设置：Stirrup 中的 GPT-5.6 Luna（medium），Keenable Search API 的 mode=realtime 与 mode=pro

➤ 定价：两种模式均为每 1000 次请求 4 美元（公开按量价格）

➤ Cards：测试中关闭了 Keenable 的 search cards（disable_cards=true）

原文：Keenable Search 1.0 takes 1st (pro, 83) and 2nd (realtime, 82) on the Artificial Analysis Search Index: ahead of Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) and Perplexity (medium) at 80

We benchmarked both modes of @KeenableAI's Keenable Search 1.0, pro and realtime, in our Stirrup agent harness with Keenable Search as its web_search tool to complete 1700 search-focused tasks.

Key benchmarking results for Keenable Search 1.0:

➤ Artificial Analysis Search Index: Keenable (pro) scores 83 and Keenable (realtime) 82, up from 67 for both modes when we first benchmarked Keenable in August, and a ~50-point lift over the same model with no search (33). Both lead Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) and Perplexity (medium) at 80

➤ DeepSearchQA is Keenable's strongest benchmark: both modes score 88 F1, 1st and 2nd of 26, ahead of Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) at 86. Pro also ranks 1st on BrowseComp (87%, level with Perplexity (medium)) and on AA-Omniscience (73%, level with Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) and Firecrawl)

➤ Fast search: realtime takes 2.3s of search time per task (2nd of 26) and pro 3.0s (3rd), against a board median of 12.5s. Each search returns in about 330ms (realtime) and 390ms (pro) on average

Key details:

➤ Setup: GPT-5.6 Luna (medium) in Stirrup, Keenable Search API with mode=realtime and mode=pro

➤ Pricing: $4 per 1k requests in both modes (public pay-as-you-go price)

➤ Cards: We disabled Keenable's search cards for testing (disable_cards=true)

@teortaxesTex（保留 40 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 187 条）

他们客观上拥有更强的模型，而且这些模型是“可发布的”，意思是已在内部大规模使用。在 GPT-4 之前的时代，私有与公开的能力差距大约是 6 个月。到 2025 年，它大概缩小到了 3 个月。现在我认为它又在拉大。

2026-11-11 18:12 北京时间 ｜ Source: @teortaxesTex ｜ 原帖链接

首先，这是个错觉。其实我对短时间线比以往任何时候都更确信。我只是在想自己可能错在哪里，然后脑海里冒出某种直觉。
这*确实*关乎例行的突破。我可以看到 AI 会非常快地补上能力的最后一公里，比以前更快，而不必变成普遍的超人类智能，就像它们当初补上自然语言的流畅性一样。它们正在径直烧穿 LeCun 的 divergence 幻想，就像当初烧穿 Chomsky 的语法批评一样。如果我们还在做 METR 时间跨度测量，现在大概已经是以周计了。连小模型也在到达那里。这不是缩放定律和对数进步的动态。这是超指数的，就像培养皿里酵母繁殖、吞食糖分时糖浓度下降的速度。到某个时点糖会耗尽；它们只是在所覆盖的领域里发展出 100% 无差错运行，就像它们发展出可以无限生成连贯语法文本的能力。如果它们泛化得好，那就完了，终局已至，因为那个领域会是“完全一般性的算法推理与问题解决”。但我仍然看到它们比如在狭窄环境上严重过拟合（用 Franka Panda 默认设置做机器人控制就是个好例子），以及在被忽视的语言上，不同版本之间来回摇摆。这让我担心整条路子太窄了。

不知道还能说什么，该睡觉了

原文：First, false impression. I am actually more short-timeline-pilled than ever. I’m just thinking how I might be wrong, and a certain intuition comes to mind.
It *is* about routine breakthroughs. I can see AIs covering the last mile in competence VERY quickly, quicker than before, without becoming generally superhumanly smart, just like they did with fluency in the natural language. They are burning straight through LeCun’s divergence fantasy like they burned through Chomsky’s grammar critiques. If we were still doing METR time horizons they would have been measured in weeks. Even small models are getting there. This isn’t a dynamic of scaling laws and logarithmic progress. It’s superexponential, like the speed of the decay in sugar concentration as the yeast multiplies and devours it in a Petri dish. At some point sugar runs out; they just develop 100% error free operation in the covered domain like they developed the ability to indefinitely generate coherent grammatical text. If they generalized well, that’s be it, the endgame, because the domain would have been “algorithmic reasoning and problem-solving in full generality”. But I still see them eg heavily overfit to narrow envs (robotic control with Franka Panda default is a good example) and flip-flopping in neglected languages between releases. It makes me worry that this entire approach is too narrow.

Idk what else to say, need to sleep所回复原帖（@leothecurious）：i've noticed u've shifted ur priors considerably in the direction of skepticism over the past month. i think it's probably more grounded now than what yesteryear you would've argued, but i'm curious what makes u think this *now*, especially at peak hype and routine breakthrough announcements? 链接

Adding clean data to your dataset is assumed to weaken poison data. We show the opposite can occur. Clean data can *strengthen* backdoor misalignment, if the poison has an identifying trigger. We call this homeopathic learning. https://t.co/Ameebo0g7o 链接

X 上就没什么商业化，除了拿点马斯克低保。

那天生财线下分享我也提到了，x 的变现方式不多，且天花板不高。

创作者收益是性价比并不高，商单环境也不如国内平台，剩下的就是私域变现，这个重要还是看受众，你的产品在能上x的这个群体里能不能有人为你付费。

大部分人想搞的产品，在x上是赚不到钱的，更多是吸引同行。

综合下来，x更适合搞社交，真赚钱还得去抖音小红书玩成年人的游戏。

引用原帖（@?）：x的简中区看似个个都在喊搞钱，其实没什么“钱气”。

跟抖音那种极致商业化的氛围不一样，也不同于小红书那样端着卖。就连视频号这种商业化程度偏弱的平台，“钱气”的流动也比这里浓。

他们正在打造一个面向卡车和重型设备的市场，从消防车到垃圾车，这些设备维系着社区运转。

一辆新消防车可能要 200 万美元、还要等上数年，而一个部门卖掉旧车时，往往只能在本地拍卖得到接近废铁的价格。Garage 把全国买卖双方连在一起，超过 90% 的销售跨州，并能按邮编即时给出运费报价。

西弗吉尼亚州南查尔斯顿去年从多余资产出售中预算了几十万美元，通过 Garage 进账 120 万美元，足以资助替换设备和消防队员的癌症筛查。

全美 50 个州已有超过 1000 个社区使用 Garage，设备年销售额超过 1 亿美元。

原文：Congrats to @alazsengul, @MartinHuntJr, and Garage (W24) on their $41M Series B!

They’re building the marketplace for the trucks and heavy equipment that keep communities running, from fire engines to garbage trucks.

A new fire truck can cost $2M and take years to arrive, while a department selling its old one often got scrap prices at a local auction. Garage connects buyers and sellers nationwide, with more than 90% of sales crossing state lines and instant freight quotes by ZIP code.

South Charleston, West Virginia budgeted a few hundred thousand dollars from surplus sales last year and brought in $1.2M through Garage, enough to fund replacement equipment and cancer screenings for its firefighters.
